Abstract: This position paper argues that recent progress with diversity in NLP is disproportionately concentrated on a small number of areas surrounding fairness. We further argue that this is the result of a number of incentives, biases, and barriers which come together to disenfranchise marginalized researchers in non-fairness fields, or to move them into fairness-related fields. We substantiate our claims with an investigation into the demographics of NLP researchers by subfield, using our research to support a number of recommendations for ensuring that all areas within NLP can become more inclusive and equitable. In particular, we highlight the importance of breaking down feedback loops that reinforce disparities, and the need to address geographical and linguistic barriers that hinder participation in NLP research.

</details>


### [37] [CausalDetox: Causal Head Selection and Intervention for Language Model Detoxification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14602)
*Yian Wang,Yuen Chen,Agam Goyal,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAUSALDETOX利用因果推断中的必要且充分概率(PNS)识别生成有毒内容的注意力头，通过推理时干预和微调两种策略进行解毒，在多个数据集上实现更好的毒性降低效果，同时保持语言流利度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常生成有毒内容，带来安全部署风险。现有缓解策略要么降低生成质量，要么需要昂贵的人工标注。

Method: 1. 使用PNS（必要且充分概率）因果分析方法识别导致毒性生成的注意力头；2. 开发两种干预策略：推理时干预（动态输入特定 steering vectors）和PNS引导微调（永久消除毒性表示）；3. 提出PARATOX基准数据集（对齐的有毒/非毒句子对）。

Result: 在ToxiGen、ImplicitHate和ParaDetox数据集上，CAUSALDETOX比基线方法实现高达5.34%的毒性降低，同时保持语言流利度，头选择速度提升7倍。

Conclusion: 通过因果分析识别关键注意力头并针对性干预，可有效实现LLM解毒，且兼具高效性和质量保持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate toxic content, posing significant risks for safe deployment. Current mitigation strategies often degrade generation quality or require costly human annotation. We propose CAUSALDETOX, a framework that identifies and intervenes on the specific attention heads causally responsible for toxic generation. Using the Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we isolate a minimal set of heads that are necessary and sufficient for toxicity. We utilize these components via two complementary strategies: (1) Local Inference-Time Intervention, which constructs dynamic, input-specific steering vectors for context-aware detoxification, and (2) PNS-Guided Fine-Tuning, which permanently unlearns toxic representations. We also introduce PARATOX, a novel benchmark of aligned toxic/non-toxic sentence pairs enabling controlled counterfactual evaluation. Experiments on ToxiGen, ImplicitHate, and ParaDetox show that CAUSALDETOX achieves up to 5.34% greater toxicity reduction compared to baselines while preserving linguistic fluency, and offers a 7x speedup in head selection.

</details>


### [38] [Retrieve, Then Classify: Corpus-Grounded Automation of Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14616)
*Sumit Mukherjee,Juan Shu,Nairwita Mazumder,Tate Kernell,Celena Wheeler,Shannon Hastings,Chris Sidey-Gibbons*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RASC（检索增强集合补全）方法，通过从语料库检索相似值集形成候选池，再对候选代码进行分类，以解决临床价值集创作中的瓶颈问题。实验表明，该方法在11,803个VSAC值集上显著优于GPT-4o零样本基线，且能有效减少无关候选数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值集创作——识别定义临床概念的标准词汇表中的所有代码——是临床质量测量和表型分析中的 recurring bottleneck。现有大型语言模型无法可靠记忆大型、结构化且版本控制的临床词汇表。

Method: 提出检索增强集合补全（RASC）方法：首先从语料库检索K个最相似的现有价值集形成候选池，然后对每个候选代码应用分类器。理论上，检索-选择策略可通过将输出空间从完整词汇表缩减到较小的候选池来降低统计复杂度。在11,803个公开可用的VSAC价值集上构建首个大规模基准测试。

Result: 基于SAPBert微调的cross-encoder达到AUROC~0.852，value-set级别F1~0.298，优于三层MLP（AUROC~0.799, F1~0.250）。两种方法都将每个真阳性的无关候选数从12.3（仅检索）减少到约3.2和4.4。零样本GPT-4o的F1仅为0.105，48.6%的返回代码不在VSAC中。随着价值集规模增大，性能差距扩大，符合RASC的理论优势。

Conclusion: RASC通过检索-选择策略有效降低了输出空间复杂度，在临床价值集创作任务上显著优于纯LLM方法。该方法在cross-encoder、SAPBert初始化cross-encoder和LightGBM等多种分类器上均表现出性能提升，证明其 benefits 跨模型类别泛化。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Clinical value set authoring -- the task of identifying all codes in a standardized vocabulary that define a clinical concept -- is a recurring bottleneck in clinical quality measurement and phenotyping. A natural approach is to prompt a large language model (LLM) to generate the required codes directly, but structured clinical vocabularies are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized during pretraining. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC): retrieve the $K$ most similar existing value sets from a curated corpus to form a candidate pool, then apply a classifier to each candidate code. Theoretically, retrieve-and-select can reduce statistical complexity by shrinking the effective output space from the full vocabulary to a much smaller retrieved candidate pool. We demonstrate the utility of RASC on 11,803 publicly available VSAC value sets, constructing the first large-scale benchmark for this task. A cross-encoder fine-tuned on SAPBert achieves AUROC~0.852 and value-set-level F1~0.298, outperforming a simpler three-layer Multilayer Perceptron (AUROC~0.799, F1~0.250) and both reduce the number of irrelevant candidates per true positive from 12.3 (retrieval-only) to approximately 3.2 and 4.4 respectively. Zero-shot GPT-4o achieves value-set-level F1~0.105, with 48.6\% of returned codes absent from VSAC entirely. This performance gap widens with increasing value set size, consistent with RASC's theoretical advantage. We observe similar performance gains across two other classifier model types, namely a cross-encoder initialized from pre-trained SAPBert and a LightGBM model, demonstrating that RASC's benefits extend beyond a single model class. The code to download and create the benchmark dataset, as well as the model training code is available at: \href{https://github.com/mukhes3/RASC}{https://github.com/mukhes3/RASC}.

</details>


### [39] [StoryCoder: Narrative Reformulation for Structured Reasoning in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14631)
*Geonhui Jang,Dongyoon Han,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryCoder框架，通过将代码生成问题转化为包含任务概述、约束条件和测试用例示例的连贯自然语言叙述，提升代码生成质量。在11个模型上的实验表明，该方法在HumanEval、LiveCodeBench和CodeForForce上实现了平均18.7%的零样本pass@10提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法主要通过增强推理步骤或注入特定思维结构来改进，但未改变问题表述本身。作者受人类将碎片化信息组织为连贯解释的方式启发，认为需要一种结构化的方法来重新表述代码生成问题。

Method: 提出StoryCoder框架，包含三个核心组件：任务概述、约束条件和测试用例示例。问题叙述由选定的算法和类型引导，形成比简单改写更丰富的上下文结构。

Result: 在11个模型上跨HumanEval、LiveCodeBench和CodeForces的实验显示：零样本pass@10平均提升18.7%。叙述重构能够引导模型采用正确的算法策略、减少实现错误、产生更模块化的代码结构。这些收益取决于叙述的连贯性和类型对齐程度。

Conclusion: 结构化问题表示对代码生成很重要，且该重要性与模型规模或架构无关。叙述重构的收益依赖于其连贯性和与任务类型的对齐程度。

Abstract: Effective code generation requires both model capability and a problem representation that carefully structures how models reason and plan. Existing approaches augment reasoning steps or inject specific structure into how models think, but leave scattered problem conditions unchanged. Inspired by the way humans organize fragmented information into coherent explanations, we propose StoryCoder, a narrative reformulation framework that transforms code generation questions into coherent natural language narratives, providing richer contextual structure than simple rephrasings. Each narrative consists of three components: a task overview, constraints, and example test cases, guided by the selected algorithm and genre. Experiments across 11 models on HumanEval, LiveCodeBench, and CodeForces demonstrate consistent improvements, with an average gain of 18.7% in zero-shot pass@10. Beyond accuracy, our analyses reveal that narrative reformulation guides models toward correct algorithmic strategies, reduces implementation errors, and induces a more modular code structure. The analyses further show that these benefits depend on narrative coherence and genre alignment, suggesting that structured problem representation is important for code generation regardless of model scale or architecture. Our code is available at https://github.com/gu-ni/StoryCoder.

</details>


### [40] [Pushing the Boundaries of Multiple Choice Evaluation to One Hundred Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14634)
*Nahyun Lee,Guijin Son*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对传统多项选择评估易被捷径策略欺骗的问题，提出将选项扩展至100的大规模选项评估框架。通过韩语拼写错误检测任务验证，发现强模型在低选项设置下的表现存在高估，高选项密度下揭示了语义混淆和位置偏好两种失败模式，且主要瓶颈为候选排名能力。该框架可作为测试模型极端干扰下可靠性的通用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多项选择评估在低选项设置下准确率接近天花板时，模型可通过捷径策略隐藏真实能力，导致对模型competence的高估。

Method: 采用大规模选项评估协议，将候选集扩展至100个选项，通过固定目标、重复重采样和随机打乱来获得稳定估计。设置padding控制和长度匹配的测试以隔离上下文长度的影响。

Result: 发现在低选项设置下的强表现可能高估模型能力；在高N条件下，由于密集干扰，此类优势通常减弱，揭示了传统基准难以察觉的能力差距。识别出两种失败模式：语义混淆和不确定性下对早期选项的位置偏好。测试表明主要瓶颈是候选排名而非上下文长度。

Conclusion: 大规模选项评估是一种通用框架，可用于压力测试模型在极端干扰密度下的可靠性，超越了传统低选项基准所能揭示的能力边界。

Abstract: Multiple choice evaluation is widely used for benchmarking large language models, yet near ceiling accuracy in low option settings can be sustained by shortcut strategies that obscure true competence. Therefore, we propose a massive option evaluation protocol that scales the candidate set to one hundred options and sharply reduces the impact of chance performance. We apply this framework to a Korean orthography error detection task where models must pick the single incorrect sentence from a large candidate set. With fixed targets and repeated resampling and shuffling, we obtain stable estimates while separating content driven failures from positional artifacts. Across experiments, results indicate that strong performance in low option settings can overstate model competence. This apparent advantage often weakens under dense interference at high $N$, revealing gaps that conventional benchmarks tend to obscure. We identify two failure modes, semantic confusion and position bias toward early options under uncertainty. To isolate the effect of context length, we run padding controlled and length matched tests, which suggest that the main bottleneck is candidate ranking rather than context length. Together, these findings support massive option evaluation as a general framework for stress testing model reliability under extreme distractor density, beyond what low option benchmarks can reveal.

</details>


### [41] [Fact4ac at the Financial Misinformation Detection Challenge Task: Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection via Fine-Tuning and Few-Shot Prompting of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14640)
*Cuong Hoang,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的无参考金融虚假信息检测框架，通过结合上下文学习（零样本和少样本提示）与低秩自适应（LoRA）参数高效微调，在RFC-BENCH基准上取得领先成绩（公开测试集95.4%，私有测试集96.3%），为金融NLP领域的上下文感知虚假信息检测提供了有效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融虚假信息的泛滥对市场稳定和投资者信任构成严重威胁，误导市场行为并造成信息不对称。现有方法在无外部证据或参考资料进行交叉验证的真实场景中检测能力受限，因此需要探索仅依赖内部语义理解和上下文一致性的检测方法。

Method: 基于RFC-BENCH框架，构建了一套综合解决方案：利用前沿LLM的推理能力，系统性地集成上下文学习策略（零样本和少样本提示）与参数高效微调方法LoRA，使模型能够有效对齐金融操纵话语中的细微语言特征。模型规模包括14B和32B参数版本。

Result: 在官方排行榜中荣获第一名的成绩：公开测试集准确率达95.4%，私有测试集准确率达96.3%，验证了该方法在金融虚假信息检测任务中的卓越有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，将LLM的推理能力与参数高效微调技术相结合，是实现上下文感知金融虚假信息检测的有效途径。该方法不仅在竞赛中取得领先性能，也为金融NLP领域的信息验证任务提供了新的技术路径。模型已公开可用。

Abstract: The proliferation of financial misinformation poses a severe threat to market stability and investor trust, misleading market behavior and creating critical information asymmetry. Detecting such misleading narratives is inherently challenging, particularly in real-world scenarios where external evidence or supplementary references for cross-verification are strictly unavailable. This paper presents our winning methodology for the "Reference-Free Financial Misinformation Detection" shared task. Built upon the recently proposed RFC-BENCH framework (Jiang et al. 2026), this task challenges models to determine the veracity of financial claims by relying solely on internal semantic understanding and contextual consistency, rather than external fact-checking. To address this formidable evaluation setup, we propose a comprehensive framework that capitalizes on the reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs). Our approach systematically integrates in-context learning, specifically zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies, with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to optimally align the models with the subtle linguistic cues of financial manipulation. Our proposed system demonstrated superior efficacy, successfully securing the first-place ranking on both official leaderboards. Specifically, we achieved an accuracy of 95.4% on the public test set and 96.3% on the private test set, highlighting the robustness of our method and contributing to the acceleration of context-aware misinformation detection in financial Natural Language Processing. Our models (14B and 32B) are available at https://huggingface.co/KaiNKaiho.

</details>


### [42] [CURaTE: Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14644)
*Seyun Bae,Seokhan Lee,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CURaTE方法，通过训练句子嵌入模型构建决策边界来识别需要遗忘的请求，无需修改LLM参数即可实现持续实时遗忘，优于现有方法并保持知识保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练无法预先过滤所有问题数据，导致需要训练后遗忘特定知识的方法。然而现有技术忽视连续即时操作的需求，导致更新累积时效用下降，以及敏感信息的暴露时间延长。

Method: CURaTE方法流程：(1)在设计好的数据集上训练句子嵌入模型，形成锐利的决策边界，用于判断输入提示是否对应存储的遗忘请求；(2)计算输入与遗忘请求的相似度；(3)根据相似度决定回答或返回拒绝响应；(4)避免修改语言模型参数。

Result: CURaTE不仅实现比现有方法更有效的遗忘效果，还保持近乎完美的知识保留能力（无论更新次数多少），且是唯一能够实现实时持续遗忘的方法。

Conclusion: 通过简单的句子嵌入模型方法，CURaTE成功解决了大语言模型持续遗忘学习的核心问题，在保持模型知识的同时高效处理遗忘请求，且具备实时处理能力。

Abstract: The inability to filter out in advance all potentially problematic data from the pre-training of large language models has given rise to the need for methods for unlearning specific pieces of knowledge after training. Existing techniques overlook the need for continuous and immediate action, causing them to suffer from degraded utility as updates accumulate and protracted exposure of sensitive information. To address these issues, we propose Continual Unlearning in Real Time with Ensured Preservation of LLM Knowledge (CURaTE). Our method begins by training a sentence embedding model on a dataset designed to enable the formation of sharp decision boundaries for determining whether a given input prompt corresponds to any stored forget requests. The similarity of a given input to the forget requests is then used to determine whether to answer or return a refusal response. We show that even with such a simple approach, not only does CURaTE achieve more effective forgetting than existing methods, but by avoiding modification of the language model parameters, it also maintains near perfect knowledge preservation over any number of updates and is the only method capable of continual unlearning in real-time.

</details>


### [43] [CURA: Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment for Language Model-Based Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14651)
*Sizhe Wang,Ziqi Xu,Claire Najjuuko,Charles Alba,Chenyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CURA框架，通过个体层面校准和群体感知正则化对齐临床LM的风险估计与不确定性。在MIMIC-IV数据集上的实验表明该方法能一致改善校准性能而不损害区分能力，使不确定性估计更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床语言模型(Clinical LMs)在支持从自由文本笔记中进行临床风险预测方面的应用日益增多，但其不确定性估计通常校准不良，在临床上不可靠。

Method: CURA通过双层不确定性目标进行不确定性微调：个体层面校准项将预测不确定性与其预测错误可能性对齐；群体感知正则化器将风险估计拉向嵌入空间中局部邻域的事件发生率，并对决策边界附近的模糊群体施加额外权重。该群体感知项可解释为带邻域信息的软标签交叉熵损失。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV临床风险预测任务上，CURA在多种临床LMs上始终改善了校准指标，且未显著损害区分能力。分析表明CURA减少了过度自信的虚假保证，为下游临床决策支持产生了更可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CURA provides a practical solution for improving uncertainty calibration in clinical LMs, enabling more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support applications.

Abstract: Clinical language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to support clinical risk prediction from free-text notes, yet their uncertainty estimates often remain poorly calibrated and clinically unreliable. In this work, we propose Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment (CURA), a framework that aligns clinical LM-based risk estimates and uncertainty with both individual error likelihoods and cohort-level ambiguities. CURA first fine-tunes domain-specific clinical LMs to obtain task-adapted patient embeddings, and then performs uncertainty fine-tuning of a multi-head classifier using a bi-level uncertainty objective. Specifically, an individual-level calibration term aligns predictive uncertainty with each patient's likelihood of error, while a cohort-aware regularizer pulls risk estimates toward event rates in their local neighborhoods in the embedding space and places extra weight on ambiguous cohorts near the decision boundary. We further show that this cohort-aware term can be interpreted as a cross-entropy loss with neighborhood-informed soft labels, providing a label-smoothing view of our method. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-IV clinical risk prediction tasks across various clinical LMs show that CURA consistently improves calibration metrics without substantially compromising discrimination. Further analysis illustrates that CURA reduces overconfident false reassurance and yields more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support.

</details>


### [44] [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672)
*Binxian Su,Haoye Lou,Shucheng Zhu,Weikang Wang,Ying Liu,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPAGBias框架，首个系统评估大语言模型空间性别偏见的工具，结合62个城市微观空间分类法、提示库和三层诊断方法。研究发现LLM中的性别-空间关联远超现实世界分布，且这种偏见嵌入预训练、指令微调和奖励建模全过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空间中性别等级制度根深蒂固，而LLM可能再现或放大这些偏见。现有偏见研究缺乏空间维度的系统分析框架，需要专门工具诊断LLM如何编码社会性别认知。

Method: 构建SPAGBias框架，包含62种城市微观空间分类体系、提示库，以及三层诊断：显性层（强制选择重采样）、概率层（token级不对称性）、建构层（语义和叙事角色分析）。对六种代表性模型进行测试，并设计故事生成实验追踪情感、措辞和社会角色的联合影响。

Result: 发现超越公私二分法的结构性性别-空间关联，形成细微的微观层面映射。偏见模式嵌入并强化于模型全流程（预训练、指令微调、奖励建模），模型关联显著超出现实世界分布。下游实验表明这些偏见在规范性和描述性应用场景中均造成实质性失败。

Conclusion: 本研究将社会学会理论与计算分析结合，将偏见研究拓展至空间领域，揭示了LLM如何通过语言编码社会性别认知。提示设计、温度和模型规模均影响偏见表达。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in urban planning, but since gendered space theory highlights how gender hierarchies are embedded in spatial organization, there is concern that LLMs may reproduce or amplify such biases. We introduce SPAGBias - the first systematic framework to evaluate spatial gender bias in LLMs. It combines a taxonomy of 62 urban micro-spaces, a prompt library, and three diagnostic layers: explicit (forced-choice resampling), probabilistic (token-level asymmetry), and constructional (semantic and narrative role analysis). Testing six representative models, we identify structured gender-space associations that go beyond the public-private divide, forming nuanced micro-level mappings. Story generation reveals how emotion, wording, and social roles jointly shape "spatial gender narratives". We also examine how prompt design, temperature, and model scale influence bias expression. Tracing experiments indicate that these patterns are embedded and reinforced across the model pipeline (pre-training, instruction tuning, and reward modeling), with model associations found to substantially exceed real-world distributions. Downstream experiments further reveal that such biases produce concrete failures in both normative and descriptive application settings. This work connects sociological theory with computational analysis, extending bias research into the spatial domain and uncovering how LLMs encode social gender cognition through language.

</details>


### [45] [Which bird does not have wings: Negative-constrained KGQA with Schema-guided Semantic Matching and Self-directed Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14749)
*Midan Shim,Seokju Hwang,Kaehyun Um,Kyong-Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NEST-KGQA任务来解决知识图谱问答中负向约束被忽视的问题，同时设计PyLF逻辑形式和CUCKOO框架来处理包含多个约束的问题，在少样本设置下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识图谱问答基准和方法偏向于正向和计算约束，忽略了负向约束（如"不包括"、"不是"等），但这类约束在实际问题中频繁出现。此外，NEST问题天然包含多个约束，现有逻辑形式难以清晰表达否定语义，且多约束带来语义复杂性挑战。

Method: 1) 提出NEST-KGQA新任务及对应数据集NestKGQA；2) 设计PyLF（Python格式化逻辑形式），清晰表达否定语义；3) 提出CUCKOO框架，采用约束感知的逻辑形式草稿生成和模式引导的语义匹配，仅在执行结果为空时选择性进行自导向细化。

Result: 实验结果表明，CUCKOO在常规KGQA和NEST-KGQA基准上均优于基线方法，尤其在少样本设置下表现突出。

Conclusion: 负向约束是KGQA中不可忽视的重要类型；PyLF能有效表达否定语义；CUCKOO框架通过选择性细化机制在保证鲁棒性的同时降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with faithfulness and hallucinations despite their remarkable reasoning abilities. In Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA), semantic parsing-based approaches address the limitations by understanding constraints in a user's question and converting them into a logical form to execute on a knowledge graph. However, existing KGQA benchmarks and methods are biased toward positive and calculation constraints. Negative constraints are neglected, although they frequently appear in real-world questions. In this paper, we introduce a new task, NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA, where each question contains at least one negative constraint, and a corresponding dataset, NestKGQA. We also design PyLF, a Python-formatted logical form, since existing logical forms are hardly suitable to express negation clearly while maintaining readability. Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [46] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CoPA基准，用于评估大语言模型在问答任务中的个性化能力，通过挖掘社区-个体偏好差异(CIPD)，提取六个关键个性化因素作为评估维度，并构建包含1,985个用户画像的测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化QA评估方法主要依赖词汇级相似性或人工启发式规则，缺乏数据驱动的验证，导致无法有效评估模型对个体用户认知偏好的对齐程度。

Method: 1) 挖掘社区-个体偏好差异(CIPD)，即个体选择与群体共识相悖的情况；2) 从CIPD中提炼六个关键个性化因素作为评估维度；3) 通过用户交互模式推断其认知偏好；4) 构建包含1,985个用户画像的CoPA基准。

Result: CoPA能够量化模型输出与用户特定认知偏好之间的对齐程度，提供比通用指标更全面、更具区分性的个性化QA评估标准。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个新的细粒度、因子级个性化QA评估范式，为LLM个性化能力评估提供了数据驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [47] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MM-AQA基准通过系统变换揭示当前多模态模型在证据不足时难以有效弃权的根本问题。研究表明MAS虽有帮助但核心挑战是模型校准而非架构设计，且现有提示策略不足，需专门训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM和MAS评估范式假设问题总是可回答的，这与现实应用场景不符。文本模态下的弃权机制已有研究，但多模态场景下未被充分探索。现有基准忽略不可回答性或使用粗粒度方法，无法捕捉实际失败模式。

Method: 构建MM-AQA基准，包含2079个样本，通过沿两个维度（视觉依赖性和证据充分性）对可回答问题进行变换来构建不可回答实例。三种前沿VLM（闭源和开源）和两种MAS架构的评估。

Result: 主要发现：(1)标准提示下VLMs很少主动弃权，简单置信度基线反而优于该设置；(2)MAS提升弃权能力但引入准确率-弃权权衡；(3)顺序设计匹配或超越迭代变体，表明瓶颈在误校准而非推理深度；(4)模型仅在证据完全缺失时弃权，但对降质或矛盾证据仍尝试调和。

Conclusion: Achieving effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than improved prompting strategies or additional agent components. The core bottleneck is model miscalibration rather than reasoning depth limitations.

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [48] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将LLM遗忘学习重新定义为非对称双任务问题，以保留为主目标、遗忘为辅助目标，提出保留优先梯度合成框架，并实例化PCGrad和SAGO两种方法。SAGO通过构造性符号约束实现更紧密的梯度对齐，在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准上显著优于基线，如WMDP Bio上MMLU性能从44.6%提升至96.0%。核心发现是：重塑梯度几何结构而非重新平衡损失才是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM遗忘学习面临目标冲突：移除特定知识的同时需保留通用能力。现有方法多通过损失重平衡解决，但效果有限。本文认为关键在于梯度几何结构的重塑，而非简单的损失平衡。

Method: 提出保留优先梯度合成框架，将任务特定梯度提取与冲突感知组合解耦。实例化两种变体：(1)适配PCGrad解决梯度冲突；(2)提出SAGO方法，通过构造性符号约束实现更严格的保留梯度对齐。

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准上，SAGO持续推进Pareto前沿。以WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD)为例，目标模型MMLU性能从44.6%(朴素方法)→94.0%(+PCGrad)→96.0%(+SAGO)，同时保持相当的遗忘强度。

Conclusion: 梯度几何结构的重塑是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键，而非损失重平衡。SAGO通过构造性符号约束实现更优的保留-遗忘平衡，为LLM遗忘学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果

Conclusion: 未在摘要中明确给出

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [50] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将行为特征（BP）标注视为一组标注技能的集合而非单一任务，评估了大语言模型在BP标注中的可行性。通过30个汉语隐喻色彩词衍生词的3,134条检索线实例和14特征BP标注方案，发现GPT-5.4在5项直接可操作技能上达到中等可靠性（准确率0.678），但整体标注能力高度异质，无法实现全局自动化。研究提出"共享分类、独立执行"模式，建议自动标注评估应以技能可行性而非任务级自动化为导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BP标注涉及多语言维度的同步编码，难以整体自动化。当前研究多将标注视为单一任务进行评估，忽略了标注技能的异质性。需要从技能分解视角重新审视LLM辅助标注的可行性。

Method: 采用技能文件驱动的流水线框架，将14个BP特征外部化为标注方案文件、决策规则和示例。300实例验证集经两轮仅方案标注协议，两位人类标注者完成标注。基于此将BP技能分类为：直接可操作、可通过聚焦重标注恢复、结构性未明确三类。GPT-5.4及三个本地部署开源模型在相同设置下进行评估。

Result: GPT-5.4在保留技能上达到中等可靠性（准确率0.678，κ=0.665，加权F1=0.695）。14项技能中5项直接可操作，4项可通过聚焦重标注恢复，5项结构性未明确。人机困难谱在技能层面高度相关（r=0.881），但在实例层面（r=0.016）和词项层面（r=-0.142）不相关，呈现"共享分类、独立执行"特征。开源模型失败集中于方案到技能的转换环节。

Conclusion: BP标注的自动化评估应从任务级转向技能级。GPT-5.4更适合作独立的第三标注声音而非人类标注的替代者。技能级评估框架有助于识别LLM在复杂语言标注任务中的选择性可行性，为设计混合人机标注流程提供依据。

Abstract: Behavioral Profile (BP) annotation is difficult to automate because it requires simultaneous coding across multiple linguistic dimensions. We treat BP annotation as a bundle of annotation skills rather than a single task and evaluate LLM-assisted BP annotation from this perspective. Using 3,134 concordance lines of 30 Chinese metaphorical color-term derivatives and a 14-feature BP schema, we implement a skill-file-driven pipeline in which each feature is externally defined through schema files, decision rules, and examples. Two human annotators completed a two-round schema-only protocol on a 300-instance validation subset, enabling BP skills to be classified as directly operable, recoverable under focused re-annotation, or structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 and three locally deployable open-source models were then evaluated under the same setup. Results show that BP annotation is highly heterogeneous at the skill level: 5 skills are directly operable, 4 are recoverable after focused re-annotation, and 5 remain structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 executes the retained skills with substantial reliability (accuracy = 0.678, \k{appa} = 0.665, weighted F1 = 0.695), but this feasibility is selective rather than global. Human and GPT difficulty profiles are strongly aligned at the skill level (r = 0.881), but not at the instance level (r = 0.016) or lexical-item level (r = -0.142), a pattern we describe as shared taxonomy, independent execution. Pairwise agreement further suggests that GPT is better understood as an independent third skill voice than as a direct human substitute. Open-source failures are concentrated in schema-to-skill execution problems. These findings suggest that automatic annotation should be evaluated in terms of skill feasibility rather than task-level automation.

</details>

Abstract: Understanding climate change requires reasoning over complex causal networks. Yet, existing causal discovery datasets predominantly capture explicit, direct causal relations. We introduce ClimateCause, a manually expert-annotated dataset of higher-order causal structures from science-for-policy climate reports, including implicit and nested causality. Cause-effect expressions are normalized and disentangled into individual causal relations to facilitate graph construction, with unique annotations for cause-effect correlation, relation type, and spatiotemporal context. We further demonstrate ClimateCause's value for quantifying readability based on the semantic complexity of causal graphs underlying a statement. Finally, large language model benchmarking on correlation inference and causal chain reasoning highlights the latter as a key challenge.

### [52] [Segment-Level Coherence for Robust Harmful Intent Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14865)
*Xuanli He,Bilgehan Sel,Faizan Ali,Jenny Bao,Hoagy Cunningham,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型（LLM）在高风险CBRN（化学、生物、辐射、核）领域中的自适应越狱攻击检测方法。通过改进流式探测目标，要求多个证据 token 持续支持预测而非依赖单点高分数token，显著提升了检测性能。在1%误报率下，真阳性率相对提升35.55%；即使基线AUROC已达97.40%，仍有实质性改进。注意力层或MLP激活层的探测效果优于残差流特征。该方法还能以"即插即用"方式检测基于对抗微调的新型字符级密码攻击，AUROC超过98.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式探测方法在高风险CBRN领域的LLM监控中存在系统性错误，主要原因在于依赖少数高分数token进行判断，导致敏感CBRN词汇出现在良性语境时产生误报。需要在保证低误报率的同时提升有害意图检测能力。

Method: 提出一种新的流式探测目标，要求多个证据token持续支持预测而非依赖孤立的高分token尖峰。鼓励基于聚合信号而非单token线索的更鲁棒检测。研究对比了注意力层、MLP层和残差流特征的探测效果。

Result: 在1%固定误报率下，真阳性率相对提升35.55%。AUROC有实质性提升，即使基线性能已接近饱和（AUROC=97.40%）。注意力层或MLP激活特征的探测效果一致优于残差流特征。对抗微调生成的字符级密码攻击仍可检测，AUROC超过98.85%。

Conclusion: 基于多证据聚合的流式探测方法能有效提升LLM在高风险CBRN领域的有害内容检测能力，具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化性，可作为"即插即用"模块应用于各种LLM的安全防护。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly exposed to adaptive jailbreaking, particularly in high-stakes Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear (CBRN) domains. Although streaming probes enable real-time monitoring, they still make systematic errors. We identify a core issue: existing methods often rely on a few high-scoring tokens, leading to false alarms when sensitive CBRN terms appear in benign contexts. To address this, we introduce a streaming probing objective that requires multiple evidence tokens to consistently support a prediction, rather than relying on isolated spikes. This encourages more robust detection based on aggregated signals instead of single-token cues. At a fixed 1% false-positive rate, our method improves the true-positive rate by 35.55% relative to strong streaming baselines. We further observe substantial gains in AUROC, even when starting from near-saturated baseline performance (AUROC = 97.40%). We also show that probing Attention or MLP activations consistently outperforms residual-stream features. Finally, even when adversarial fine-tuning enables novel character-level ciphers, harmful intent remains detectable: probes developed for the base LLMs can be applied ``plug-and-play'' to these obfuscated attacks, achieving an AUROC of over 98.85%.

</details>


### [53] [Reasoning Dynamics and the Limits of Monitoring Modality Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14888)
*Danae Sánchez Villegas,Samuel Lewis-Lim,Nikolaos Aletras,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文分析了18个视觉语言模型（VLM）的推理动态过程，揭示了模型在链式思考（CoT）推理中存在的"答案惯性"现象，以及推理训练对模型纠正行为和模态依赖的复杂影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型的推理能力虽有所提升，但其如何整合视觉和文本信息进行推理的内在机制尚不清楚，需要系统性地探究推理过程中的信息融合与决策机制。

Method: 研究选取了18个VLM（包括指令微调模型和推理训练模型），通过跟踪链式思考过程中的置信度变化、测量推理的纠正效果、评估中间推理步骤的贡献，并采用控制性干预实验（引入误导性文本线索）来评估模型对不同模态信息的依赖程度。

Result: 1）模型普遍存在"答案惯性"——早期预测在推理过程中被强化而非修正；2）推理训练模型表现出更强的纠正行为，但其效果依赖于模态条件（从文本主导到纯视觉设置）；3）模型始终受到误导性文本线索的影响，即使视觉证据已足够充分；4）推理训练模型更可能在CoT中显式引用文本线索，但其流畅且较长的推理过程可能看似视觉 grounded，实则暗含文本依赖；5）指令微调模型的CoT较短，显露出与视觉输入的不一致性。

Conclusion: 链式思考仅提供了多模态驱动VLM决策的部分视角，CoT的表面一致性可能掩盖真实的模态依赖模式，这对多模态系统的透明度和安全性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) offer reasoning capabilities, yet how these unfold and integrate visual and textual information remains unclear. We analyze reasoning dynamics in 18 VLMs covering instruction-tuned and reasoning-trained models from two different model families. We track confidence over Chain-of-Thought (CoT), measure the corrective effect of reasoning, and evaluate the contribution of intermediate reasoning steps. We find that models are prone to answer inertia, in which early commitments to a prediction are reinforced, rather than revised during reasoning steps. While reasoning-trained models show stronger corrective behavior, their gains depend on modality conditions, from text-dominant to vision-only settings. Using controlled interventions with misleading textual cues, we show that models are consistently influenced by these cues even when visual evidence is sufficient, and assess whether this influence is recoverable from CoT. Although this influence can appear in the CoT, its detectability varies across models and depends on what is being monitored. Reasoning-trained models are more likely to explicitly refer to the cues, but their longer and fluent CoTs can still appear visually grounded while actually following textual cues, obscuring modality reliance. In contrast, instruction-tuned models refer to the cues less explicitly, but their shorter traces reveal inconsistencies with the visual input. Taken together, these findings indicate that CoT provides only a partial view of how different modalities drive VLM decisions, with important implications for the transparency and safety of multimodal systems.

</details>


### [54] [Comparison of Modern Multilingual Text Embedding Techniques for Hate Speech Detection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14907)
*Evaldas Vaiciukynas,Paulius Danenas,Linas Ablonskis,Algirdas Sukys,Edgaras Dambrauskas,Voldemaras Zitkus,Rita Butkiene,Rimantas Butleris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了现代多语言句子嵌入模型在立陶宛语、俄语和英语中进行仇恨言论检测的能力，并引入了一个新的立陶宛语仇恨言论语料库LtHate。研究表明，监督式双分类器始终优于单类异常检测，结合梯度提升决策树的多语言嵌入可实现高达92.19%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线仇恨言论和滥用语言对内容审核构成日益严峻的挑战，尤其是在多语言环境和立陶宛语等低资源语言的场景中。

Method: 引入LtHate语料库，基准测试6种多语言编码器（potion、gemma、bge、snow、jina、e5）在LtHate、RuToxic和EnSuperset数据集上的表现。采用两种下游模型（HBOS单类异常检测器和CatBoost双分类器），并测试PCA压缩至64维特征的效果。

Result: 监督式双分类模型始终显著优于单类异常检测。最佳配置：立陶宛语80.96%准确率/AUC 0.887（jina），俄语92.19%准确率/AUC 0.978（e5），英语77.21%准确率/AUC 0.859（e5+PCA）。PCA压缩在监督场景中保留几乎全部判别能力，但对无监督异常检测有负面影响。

Conclusion: 现代多语言句子嵌入结合梯度提升决策树为多语言仇恨言论检测提供了稳健的软计算解决方案，在低资源语言场景下同样有效。

Abstract: Online hate speech and abusive language pose a growing challenge for content moderation, especially in multilingual settings and for low-resource languages such as Lithuanian. This paper investigates to what extent modern multilingual sentence embedding models can support accurate hate speech detection in Lithuanian, Russian, and English, and how their performance depends on downstream modeling choices and feature dimensionality. We introduce LtHate, a new Lithuanian hate speech corpus derived from news portals and social networks, and benchmark six modern multilingual encoders (potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5) on LtHate, RuToxic, and EnSuperset using a unified Python pipeline. For each embedding, we train both a one class HBOS anomaly detector and a two class CatBoost classifier, with and without principal component analysis (PCA) compression to 64-dimensional feature vectors. Across all datasets, two class supervised models consistently and substantially outperform one class anomaly detection, with the best configurations achieving up to 80.96% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.887 in Lithuanian (jina), 92.19% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.978 in Russian (e5), and 77.21% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.859 in English (e5 with PCA). PCA compression preserves almost all discriminative power in the supervised setting, while showing some negative impact for the unsupervised anomaly detection case. These results demonstrate how modern multilingual sentence embeddings combined with gradient boosted decision trees provide robust soft-computing solutions for multilingual hate speech detection applications.

</details>


### [55] [IE as Cache: Information Extraction Enhanced Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14930)
*Hang Lv,Sheng Liang,Hongchao Gu,Wei Guo,Defu Lian,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IE-as-Cache框架，将信息抽取(IE)从传统的终端任务重新定位为认知缓存机制，用于增强LLM智能体的推理能力。通过模拟计算机存储层次结构，结合查询驱动的抽取与缓存感知的推理，实现中间信息的动态维护与噪声过滤。实验表明该方法能显著提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IE被视作终端任务，其抽取结果往往是孤立消费的，缺乏在多步推理中的动态维护与复用能力。这限制了IE作为可重用认知资源的潜力，导致推理过程中信息冗余与噪声干扰。

Method: 提出IE-as-Cache框架，灵感来源于计算机分层存储体系结构。具体包括：(1) 查询驱动的抽取机制——根据推理需求动态触发IE；(2) 缓存感知推理——在推理过程中动态维护紧凑的中间信息并过滤噪声。该方法无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准数据集上，针对多种LLM进行实验，结果显示推理准确率获得显著提升。

Conclusion: IE可以被有效重新定位为可重用的认知资源，IE-as-Cache为未来IE下游应用研究提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Information Extraction aims to distill structured, decision-relevant information from unstructured text, serving as a foundation for downstream understanding and reasoning. However, it is traditionally treated merely as a terminal objective: once extracted, the resulting structure is often consumed in isolation rather than maintained and reused during multi-step inference. Moving beyond this, we propose \textit{IE-as-Cache}, a framework that repurposes IE as a cognitive cache to enhance agentic reasoning. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical computer memory, our approach combines query-driven extraction with cache-aware reasoning to dynamically maintain compact intermediate information and filter noise. Experiments on challenging benchmarks across diverse LLMs demonstrate significant improvements in reasoning accuracy, indicating that IE can be effectively repurposed as a reusable cognitive resource and offering a promising direction for future research on downstream uses of IE.

</details>


### [56] [XQ-MEval: A Dataset with Cross-lingual Parallel Quality for Benchmarking Translation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14934)
*Jingxuan Liu,Zhi Qu,Jin Tei,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出XQ-MEval数据集，通过半自动方式构建九种翻译方向的评测基准，系统揭示了跨语言评分偏差问题，并提出归一化策略改善多语言度量评估的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多语言翻译系统评估依赖跨语言平均分数，但不同语言的翻译质量评分存在系统性偏差，即同等质量的译文在不同语言中获得不同分数。由于缺乏平行质量基准数据，该问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 自动向标准译文注入MQM定义的错误；2) 由母语者过滤确保可靠性；3) 合并错误生成可控质量的伪译文；4) 将伪译文与源句和参考译文组成三元组用于评估。

Result: 1) 九种代表性度量的实验揭示了平均化评分与人类判断的不一致；2) 首次提供跨语言评分偏差的实证证据；3) 基于数据集提出归一化策略，可对齐跨语言分数分布。

Conclusion: 归一化策略能有效提升多语言度量评估的公平性和可靠性，为构建更公正的翻译质量评估体系奠定基础。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are essential for building multilingual translation systems. The common practice of evaluating these systems is averaging metric scores across languages, yet this is suspicious since metrics may suffer from cross-lingual scoring bias, where translations of equal quality receive different scores across languages. This problem has not been systematically studied because no benchmark exists that provides parallel-quality instances across languages, and expert annotation is not realistic. In this work, we propose XQ-MEval, a semi-automatically built dataset covering nine translation directions, to benchmark translation metrics. Specifically, we inject MQM-defined errors into gold translations automatically, filter them by native speakers for reliability, and merge errors to generate pseudo translations with controllable quality. These pseudo translations are then paired with corresponding sources and references to form triplets used in assessing the qualities of translation metrics. Using XQ-MEval, our experiments on nine representative metrics reveal the inconsistency between averaging and human judgment and provide the first empirical evidence of cross-lingual scoring bias. Finally, we propose a normalization strategy derived from XQ-MEval that aligns score distributions across languages, improving the fairness and reliability of multilingual metric evaluation.

</details>


### [57] [Text2Arch: A Dataset for Generating Scientific Architecture Diagrams from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14941)
*Shivank Garg,Sankalp Mittal,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从文本描述自动生成科学架构图的方法。通过构建包含图像、文本描述和DOT代码的综合数据集，微调小型语言模型，实现从文本语义理解到架构图生成的任务。实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线模型，性能与GPT-4o上下文学习相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通过纯文本沟通复杂系统设计或科学流程效率低下且容易产生歧义。缺乏清洁、大规模的开源数据集，导致该任务没有有效的开放模型可用。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个名为\system{}的综合数据集，包含科学架构图像、对应文本描述及DOT代码表示。基于该数据集，研究者对一系列小型语言模型进行了微调，并采用GPT-4o进行上下文学习（in-context learning）实验。

Result: \system{}模型显著优于现有的DiagramAgent等基线模型，且在与GPT-4o基于上下文学习的生成效果对比中表现相当。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了一个综合数据集，并证明了经过微调的小型语言模型能够显著优于现有基线模型（如DiagramAgent），并与GPT-4o的上下文学习生成效果相当。所有代码、数据和模型已公开发布，为该领域提供了宝贵的资源。

Abstract: Communicating complex system designs or scientific processes through text alone is inefficient and prone to ambiguity. A system that automatically generates scientific architecture diagrams from text with high semantic fidelity can be useful in multiple applications like enterprise architecture visualization, AI-driven software design, and educational content creation. Hence, in this paper, we focus on leveraging language models to perform semantic understanding of the input text description to generate intermediate code that can be processed to generate high-fidelity architecture diagrams. Unfortunately, no clean large-scale open-access dataset exists, implying lack of any effective open models for this task. Hence, we contribute a comprehensive dataset, \system, comprising scientific architecture images, their corresponding textual descriptions, and associated DOT code representations. Leveraging this resource, we fine-tune a suite of small language models, and also perform in-context learning using GPT-4o. Through extensive experimentation, we show that \system{} models significantly outperform existing baseline models like DiagramAgent and perform at par with in-context learning-based generations from GPT-4o. We make the code, data and models publicly available.

</details>


### [58] [Explain the Flag: Contextualizing Hate Speech Beyond Censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14970)
*Jason Liartis,Eirini Kaldeli,Lambrini Gyftokosta,Eleftherios Chelioudakis,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合方法，将大语言模型（LLM）与三个新创建的专业词汇表结合，用于检测和解释英语、法语和希腊语中的仇恨言论。该系统通过两个互补管道捕获问题内容，并生成有据可查的解释。人类评估表明，该混合方法准确率高，解释质量优于纯LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动仇恨言论检测系统主要侧重于审查或删除内容，存在透明度不足、限制言论自由、缺乏有害原因解释等问题。学术界和业界亟需更透明、可解释、可问责的仇恨言论检测方法。

Method: 提出混合方法，结合LLM与三个新创建的专业词汇表。系统通过两个互补管道工作：（1）使用词汇表检测和消歧问题术语；（2）利用LLM作为上下文感知的群体目标内容评估器。最终输出融合为有据可查的解释。

Result: 人类评估表明，该混合方法具有高准确性，解释质量高，在仇恨言论检测和解释任务上优于纯LLM基线。

Conclusion: 混合方法（LLM+专业词汇表）能有效提升仇恨言论检测的准确性和解释质量，为构建更透明、可解释的在线内容审核系统提供了有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Hate, derogatory, and offensive speech remains a persistent challenge in online platforms and public discourse. While automated detection systems are widely used, most focus on censorship or removal, raising concerns for transparency and freedom of expression, and limiting opportunities to explain why content is harmful. To address these issues, explanatory approaches have emerged as a promising solution, aiming to make hate speech detection more transparent, accountable, and informative. In this paper, we present a hybrid approach that combines Large Language Models (LLMs) with three newly created and curated vocabularies to detect and explain hate speech in English, French, and Greek. Our system captures both inherently derogatory expressions tied to identity characteristics and direct group-targeted content through two complementary pipelines: one that detects and disambiguates problematic terms using the curated vocabularies, and one that leverages LLMs as context-aware evaluators of group-targeting content. The outputs are fused into grounded explanations that clarify why content is flagged. Human evaluation shows that our hybrid approach is accurate, with high-quality explanations, outperforming LLM-only baselines.

</details>


### [59] [IUQ: Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification for Long-Form Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15109)
*Haozhi Fan,Jinhao Duan,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IUQ框架，通过"提问-回答"范式量化大语言模型长文本生成中的不确定性，利用样本间一致性和样本内忠实度，在多种模型家族和规模上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长文本和自由形式生成中存在事实性错误但语义连贯的问题，现有方法在短文本或受限输出场景有效，但在复杂语言结构和多面语义场景中表现不足。

Method: 提出Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ)框架，采用interrogate-then-respond范式，结合样本间一致性和样本内忠实度两个核心维度进行不确定性量化。

Result: 在多种模型家族和规模上，IUQ在两个广泛使用的长文本生成数据集上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: IUQ框架能够提供可靠的声明级不确定性和模型忠实度度量，为长文本LLM输出的不确定性量化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), uncertainty quantification in LLM generation is a persistent challenge. Although recent approaches have achieved strong performance by restricting LLMs to produce short or constrained answer sets, many real-world applications require long-form and free-form text generation. A key difficulty in this setting is that LLMs often produce responses that are semantically coherent yet factually inaccurate, while the underlying semantics are multifaceted and the linguistic structure is complex. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ), a novel framework that leverages inter-sample consistency and intra-sample faithfulness to quantify the uncertainty in long-form LLM outputs. By utilizing an interrogate-then-respond paradigm, our method provides reliable measures of claim-level uncertainty and the model's faithfulness. Experimental results across diverse model families and model sizes demonstrate the superior performance of IUQ over two widely used long-form generation datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/louisfanhz/IUQ.

</details>


### [60] [DiscoTrace: Representing and Comparing Answering Strategies of Humans and LLMs in Information-Seeking Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15140)
*Neha Srikanth,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoTrace是一种识别信息寻求问题回答中修辞策略的方法。研究发现人类社区答案构建策略多样，而LLM缺乏这种多样性且偏好广泛回答，可指导开发更具语用意识的LLM回答器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对回答者在回应信息寻求问题时所使用修辞策略的系统性识别方法，需要理解人类社区如何构建答案以及LLM与人类的差异。

Method: DiscoTrace将答案表示为与问题解释配对的话语行为序列，在修辞结构理论(RST)分析基础上进行标注。该方法应用于9个不同人类社区的答案分析。

Result: 人类社区在答案构建上表现出多样化的偏好；而LLM即使被提示模仿特定人类社区的回答指南，仍缺乏修辞多样性；同时LLM系统性地选择广度策略，即回应人类回答者通常选择不回答的问题解释。

Conclusion: DiscoTrace为QA系统中务实型LLM回答器的开发提供了指导原则，强调需要根据语境考虑多样化的策略，而不仅仅追求回答的广度。

Abstract: We introduce DiscoTrace, a method to identify the rhetorical strategies that answerers use when responding to information-seeking questions. DiscoTrace represents answers as a sequence of question-related discourse acts paired with interpretations of the original question, annotated on top of rhetorical structure theory parses. Applying DiscoTrace to answers from nine different human communities reveals that communities have diverse preferences for answer construction. In contrast, LLMs do not exhibit rhetorical diversity in their answers, even when prompted to mimic specific human community answering guidelines. LLMs also systematically opt for breadth, addressing interpretations of questions that human answerers choose not to address. Our findings can guide the development of pragmatic LLM answerers that consider a range of strategies informed by context in QA.

</details>


### [61] [QuantCode-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Ability of Large Language Models to Generate Executable Algorithmic Trading Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15151)
*Alexey Khoroshilov,Alexey Chernysh,Orkhan Ekhtibarov,Nini Kamkia,Dmitry Zmitrovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QuantCode-Bench是一个用于评估大语言模型生成量化交易策略能力的基准测试，包含400个任务，通过多阶段pipeline评估模型在Backtrader框架下从英文描述生成可执行策略的能力。研究发现当前模型的主要瓶颈不在语法，而在交易逻辑操作化、API正确使用和语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用编程任务上表现良好，但在生成可执行量化交易策略方面的能力尚缺乏系统评估。与标准代码基准不同，交易策略生成需要同时掌握领域特定的金融逻辑、专业API知识，以及生成不仅语法正确还能在实际历史数据上产生真实交易的代码能力。

Method: 构建QuantCode-Bench基准测试，包含400个不同难度的任务，数据来源于Reddit、TradingView、StackExchange、GitHub及合成数据。通过多阶段pipeline进行评估：语法正确性检查→回测成功执行→存在实际交易→使用LLM judge进行语义对齐评估。采用两种测试设置：单轮（首次尝试必须正确生成）和多轮agentic（模型接收迭代反馈并可修复错误）。

Result: 当前模型的主要局限性与语法无关，问题集中在三个方面：正确操作化交易逻辑、正确使用API、以及遵循任务语义。模型在将自然语言描述转化为可执行且行为正确的交易策略方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 量化交易策略生成构成一类独特的领域特定代码生成任务，其成功不仅需要技术正确性，还需要自然语言描述、金融逻辑与策略在数据上的可观察行为之间的对齐。这为未来LLM在金融领域的应用提出了新的挑战和研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong performance on general-purpose programming tasks, yet their ability to generate executable algorithmic trading strategies remains underexplored. Unlike standard code benchmarks, trading-strategy generation requires simultaneous mastery of domain-specific financial logic, knowledge of a specialized API, and the ability to produce code that is not only syntactically correct but also leads to actual trades on historical data. In this work, we present QuantCode-Bench, a benchmark for the systematic evaluation of modern LLMs in generating strategies for the Backtrader framework from textual descriptions in English. The benchmark contains 400 tasks of varying difficulty collected from Reddit, TradingView, StackExchange, GitHub, and synthetic sources. Evaluation is conducted through a multi-stage pipeline that checks syntactic correctness, successful backtest execution, the presence of trades, and semantic alignment with the task description using an LLM judge. We compare state-of-the-art models in two settings: single-turn, where the strategy must be generated correctly on the first attempt, and agentic multi-turn, where the model receives iterative feedback and may repair its errors. We analyze the failure modes across different stages of the pipeline and show that the main limitations of current models are not related to syntax, but rather to the correct operationalization of trading logic, proper API usage, and adherence to task semantics. These findings suggest that trading strategy generation constitutes a distinct class of domain-specific code generation tasks in which success requires not only technical correctness, but also alignment between natural-language descriptions, financial logic, and the observable behavior of the strategy on data.

</details>


### [62] [Compressing Sequences in the Latent Embedding Space: $K$-Token Merging for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15153)
*Zihao Xu,John Harvill,Ziwei Fan,Yizhou Sun,Hao Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur significant computational and memory costs when processing long prompts, as full self-attention scales quadratically with input length. Token compression aims to address this challenge by reducing the number of tokens representing inputs. However, existing prompt-compression approaches primarily operate in token space and overlook inefficiencies in the latent embedding space. In this paper, we propose K-Token Merging, a latent-space compression framework that merges each contiguous block of K token embeddings into a single embedding via a lightweight encoder. The compressed sequence is processed by a LoRA-adapted LLM, while generation remains in the original vocabulary. Experiments on structural reasoning (Textualized Tree), sentiment classification (Amazon Reviews), and code editing (CommitPackFT) show that K-Token Merging lies on the Pareto frontier of performance vs. compression, achieving up to 75% input length reduction with minimal performance degradation.

</details>


### [63] [Fabricator or dynamic translator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15165)
*Lisa Vasileva,Karin Sim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在机器翻译中的过度生成（overgeneration）问题，这些问题不同于传统神经机器翻译中的"神经废话"，包括LLM自我解释、风险性虚构和适当解释三种类型，并提出了在商业环境中的检测策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在机器翻译中表现优异，但其生成特性导致可能过度生成内容。不同于NMT中的神经废话，这些过度生成形式多样（包括自我解释、虚构内容、合理说明等），如何准确检测和判断其性质是一个重要挑战。

Method: 文章详细阐述了作者在商业环境中探索的不同检测策略，可能包括分类方法或人工标注结合自动检测的混合方案。

Result: 研究提出了针对商业应用场景的解决方案，能够区分不同类型的过度生成，特别是当LLM能够像人类译员一样行事、为目标受众提供更易理解的内容时。

Conclusion: 有效检测LLM机器翻译中的过度生成问题对于提升商业翻译质量至关重要，区分"有害虚构"与"有益解释"是关键。

Abstract: LLMs are proving to be adept at machine translation although due to their generative nature they may at times overgenerate in various ways. These overgenerations are different from the neurobabble seen in NMT and range from LLM self-explanations, to risky confabulations, to appropriate explanations, where the LLM is able to act as a human translator would, enabling greater comprehension for the target audience. Detecting and determining the exact nature of the overgenerations is a challenging task. We detail different strategies we have explored for our work in a commercial setting, and present our results.

</details>


### [64] [From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15244)
*Kiran Purohit,Ramasuri Narayanam,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecGuard通过模型内部信号实现step级验证，采样多个草稿候选并用注意力grounding score和对数概率score的集成进行验证，解决了投机解码的错误传播问题，同时提升准确率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前投机解码以token为中心，允许错误步骤传播。现有方法通过外部奖励模型缓解此问题，但会导致额外延迟、计算开销，并限制泛化能力。

Method: SpecGuard框架在每步采样多个草稿候选并选择最一致的步骤，然后使用两个轻量级模型内部信号的集成进行验证：(i)基于注意力的 grounding score，测量对输入和先前接受步骤的归因；(ii)基于对数概率的score，捕捉token级置信度。这些信号共同决定步骤是接受还是用目标模型重新计算，实现选择性计算分配。

Result: 在多种推理基准测试中，SpecGuard将准确率提高3.6%，同时将延迟降低约11%，优于标准SD和奖励引导SD。

Conclusion: SpecGuard成功解决了投机解码中的错误传播问题，仅使用模型内部信号即实现了更好的准确率-延迟权衡。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

### [65] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Agentic Knowledge Graphs与递归爬取技术的方法，解决传统RAG在处理层级化和关联性信息时的检索不准确问题，在联邦法规数据集上实现了70%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional RAG pipelines fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies in complex enterprise documents.

Method: Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling to handle hierarchical structures and multi-hop references in enterprise document ecosystems.

Result: Achieves 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems when evaluated on the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) benchmark.

Conclusion: Provides a robust framework for navigating complex regulatory documents with superior accuracy.

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [66] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究对比了三种RAG检索架构（Vector RAG、Tree Reasoning、Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval）在金融、法律、医疗领域的性能，建立了四层查询复杂度基准，发现Tree Reasoning总体得分最高(0.900)，但无单一范式在各层均占优，建议开发自适应检索系统根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在金融文档QA中的系统性评估不足，缺乏跨域比较和针对不同查询复杂度的自适应策略研究。

Method: 实现并评估三种检索架构，通过四层查询复杂度基准测试，使用GPT-4作为LLM-as-judge进行评估，并在FinanceBench（150个专家标注问题）上进行验证。

Result: Tree Reasoning总体最高(0.900)；Vector RAG擅长多文档合成(Tier 4, 0.900)；Hybrid AHR在交叉引用(0.850)和多段落查询(0.929)表现最佳；交叉引用召回率：tree/hybrid达100% vs vector的91.7%。FinanceBench验证：Tree Reasoning 0.938、Hybrid AHR 0.901、Vector RAG 0.821，树向差距扩大至11.7%。

Conclusion: 无单一范式能在所有查询复杂度层级占主导地位；应开发自适应检索系统，根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择检索策略。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO是一个用于评估重排序器在时间动态上下文中的性能的基准测试。研究发现现有重排序器存在对旧文档的强烈偏好，即使这些文档在事实上已过时。通过指令优化框架，可以获得高达27%的Evolving Knowledge任务提升，同时保持Non-Evolving Knowledge任务的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要在静态环境中评估重排序器，无法充分评估信息动态演化下的性能。这导致关键问题被忽视：现实世界的RAG系统需要在时间上不同的证据之间做出选择，而现有方法未能解决这一挑战。

Method: 1) 构建FRESCO基准，通过将近期性查询与历史Wikipedia修订版本配对；2) 测试重排序器是否能优先选择事实性最新且语义相关的证据；3) 采用指令优化框架识别Pareto最优指令，平衡Evolving和Non-Evolving Knowledge任务。

Result: 现有重排序器存在一致的失败模式：对语义丰富但事实已过时的旧文档有强烈偏好。通过指令优化，可获得高达27%的Evolving Knowledge任务提升，同时在Non-Evolving Knowledge任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 指令优化是缓解重排序器时间偏差的有效方法。Pareto最优指令能够在事实最新性和语义相关性之间取得平衡，为动态信息环境下的RAG系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Evaluation of Agents under Simulated AI Marketplace Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14256)
*To Eun Kim,Alireza Salemi,Hamed Zamani,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: A new evaluation paradigm called "Marketplace Evaluation" is proposed to assess information access systems in competitive marketplace settings through simulation, complementing static accuracy-based benchmarks with dynamic metrics like retention and market share.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current evaluation relies on static benchmarks with accuracy-focused measures that assume systems operate in isolation, but real-world deployment involves competitive marketplaces with user switching, routing decisions, and operational constraints. This mismatch makes it difficult to predict post-deployment success and obscures competitive effects like early-adoption advantages and market dominance.

Method: Introduces "Marketplace Evaluation," a simulation-based paradigm that evaluates information access systems as participants in a competitive marketplace. By simulating repeated interactions and evolving user and agent preferences, the framework enables longitudinal evaluation with marketplace-level metrics.

Result: The framework enables longitudinal evaluation and marketplace-level metrics (e.g., retention, market share) that complement and extend beyond traditional accuracy-based metrics.

Conclusion: The paper formalizes the framework and outlines a research agenda around marketplace simulation, metrics, optimization, and adoption, with potential application to evaluation campaigns like TREC.

Abstract: Modern information access ecosystems consist of mixtures of systems, such as retrieval systems and large language models, and increasingly rely on marketplaces to mediate access to models, tools, and data, making competition between systems inherent to deployment. In such settings, outcomes are shaped not only by benchmark quality but also by competitive pressure, including user switching, routing decisions, and operational constraints. Yet evaluation is still largely conducted on static benchmarks with accuracy-focused measures that assume systems operate in isolation. This mismatch makes it difficult to predict post-deployment success and obscures competitive effects such as early-adoption advantages and market dominance. We introduce Marketplace Evaluation, a simulation-based paradigm that evaluates information access systems as participants in a competitive marketplace. By simulating repeated interactions and evolving user and agent preferences, the framework enables longitudinal evaluation and marketplace-level metrics, such as retention and market share, that complement and can extend beyond traditional accuracy-based metrics. We formalize the framework and outline a research agenda, motivated by business and economics, around marketplace simulation, metrics, optimization, and adoption in evaluation campaigns like TREC.

</details>


### [69] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对具有正式权威层级的领域（如法律、药品监管、软件安全），提出CAR（控制权威检索）新范式，解决后续文档撤销早期文档时的检索问题。证明TCA检索的理论边界，提供两阶段解决方案，显著降低RAG系统产生虚假信息的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律、药品监管、软件安全等领域，后续文档可正式撤销早期文档，但语义上可能与之相距甚远。传统检索方法（argmax_d s(q,d)）无法解决文档权威状态的动态演变问题，导致RAG系统产生虚假信息。

Method: 提出 Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR) 框架，定义TCA(R,q)评分机制；通过Theorem 4建立检索正确性的充要条件（边界包含性和无忽略覆写），通过Proposition 2证明phi(q)为硬上界；采用两阶段检索范式验证。

Result: 三个真实语料验证有效：安全公告Dense→0.975、SCOTUS推翻对→0.926、FDA药品记录→0.774；GPT-4o-mini下游实验显示Dense RAG产生39%虚假"未修补"声明，两阶段降至16%。

Conclusion: 论文提出了CAR这一新检索范式，通过TCA评分机制有效识别权威文档的当前有效边界，显著降低下游任务中的错误信息风险（从39%降至16%），并提供了完整的理论框架和实用工具。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [70] [NewsTorch: A PyTorch-based Toolkit for Learner-oriented News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14510)
*Rongyao Wang,Veronica Liesaputra,Zhiyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NewsTorch是一个基于PyTorch的新闻推荐工具包，采用模块化设计，支持GUI操作，提供数据预处理和模型训练测试功能，旨在帮助学习者掌握新闻推荐的概念和实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏专门面向学习者的新闻推荐工具包，阻碍了该领域研究的推进。

Method: 开发了基于PyTorch的模块化、解耦、可扩展的新闻推荐工具包NewsTorch，包含友好的GUI平台，支持数据集下载与预处理，提供标准化的模型训练、验证和测试流程。

Result: 该工具包可实现数据集下载与预处理，支持训练、验证和测试先进的神经新闻推荐模型，提供标准化评估指标，确保公平比较和实验可复现性。

Conclusion: 开源工具包已发布于GitHub，为新闻推荐领域的研究者和学习者提供了实用的研究和教学资源。

Abstract: News recommender systems are devised to alleviate the information overload, attracting more and more researchers' attention in recent years. The lack of a dedicated learner-oriented news recommendation toolkit hinders the advancement of research in news recommendation. We propose a PyTorch-based news recommendation toolkit called NewsTorch, developed to support learners in acquiring both conceptual understanding and practical experience. This toolkit provides a modular, decoupled, and extensible framework with a learner-friendly GUI platform that supports dataset downloading and preprocessing. It also enables training, validation, and testing of state-of-the-art neural news recommendation models with standardized evaluation metrics, ensuring fair comparison and reproducible experiments. Our open-source toolkit is released on Github: https://github.com/whonor/NewsTorch.

</details>


### [71] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec是一种面向视频游戏的新型推荐框架，通过精度驱动模块、多样性驱动模块和综合模块三部分解决现有游戏推荐系统中精度与多样性难以平衡的问题，在Steam数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频游戏推荐方法存在三个主要问题：1）过度关注精度而忽视多样性，导致推荐结果单一；2）游戏之间缺乏严格的关联建模来提升精度；3）多样性方法在邻居建模和消息传播中未能有效利用类别和流行度等关键物品信息。

Method: CPGRec框架包含三个模块：①精度驱动模块——通过更严格的方式连接游戏以提升推荐精度；②多样性驱动模块——在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居，并利用热门游戏节点放大长尾游戏的影响；③综合模块——结合前两个模块并采用负样本评分权重重调整方法平衡精度与多样性。

Result: 在Steam数据集上的实验结果表明，CPGRec能够有效提升游戏推荐效果。

Conclusion: 该框架通过创新的图连接方式和负样本重加权策略，成功在推荐精度与多样性之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [72] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: U-GLAD is an uncertainty-aware generative learning path recommendation framework that addresses representation bias in learner modeling and goal personalization through Gaussian-based cognitive state estimation, attention-driven concept encoding, and diffusion-based generative recommendation. It achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks while providing interpretable, goal-aligned learning paths.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) systems suffer from two critical limitations: (1) failure to account for historical interaction uncertainty arising from lucky guesses or accidental slips, which can misrepresent a learner's true cognitive state; (2) insufficient adaptability to diverse and personalized learning objectives.

Method: U-GLAD employs three core technical components: (1) Gaussian LSTM encoder modeling cognitive states as probability distributions to capture learners' true underlying states; (2) Goal-oriented concept encoder using multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations for dynamic alignment of concept semantics with individual learning goals; (3) Generative diffusion model to predict latent representations of optimal next concepts, departing from traditional discriminative ranking approaches.

Result: Empirical evaluation on three public datasets demonstrates that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baseline methods. Qualitative analysis further confirms its superior capability in accurately perceiving interaction uncertainty and generating stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

Conclusion: U-GLAD introduces a generative diffusion-based paradigm for educational recommendation, effectively bridging the gap between uncertain learner interactions and goal-driven path planning. The framework's ability to model cognitive states probabilistically and adapt to diverse learning objectives positions it as a robust solution for personalized education systems.

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [73] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SF-UBM是一种语义增强的联邦用户行为建模方法，用于在保护用户隐私的前提下实现跨域推荐。通过自然语言作为跨域桥梁、事实-反事实知识蒸馏模块以及语义-行为空间对齐三大技术，解决了非重叠域隐私保护跨域推荐中的对齐、模态异构和协同信号融合三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法忽视了隐私保护问题，且传统跨域推荐假设域可重叠访问。实际场景中，用户身份和行为数据无法跨域共享，导致非重叠域的隐私保护跨域推荐（PPCDR）面临三大挑战：1）无法共享用户数据导致跨域对齐困难；2）跨域数据模态异构；3）传统协同过滤信号与LLM特征空间难以融合。

Method: 提出SF-UBM方法，包含三个核心模块：1）语义增强联邦架构——以文本商品表示作为跨域桥梁，加密传输同时保留用户本地数据；2）Fact-counter知识蒸馏模块——整合域无关知识与域特定知识；3）软提示空间投影——将预学习用户偏好和跨域商品表示投影至软提示空间，对齐行为空间与语义空间。

Result: 在三个真实领域数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明SF-UBM相比最新SOTA方法具有显著有效性提升。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将LLM应用于非重叠域隐私保护跨域推荐场景，通过语义增强联邦架构有效解决了数据隔离与跨域对齐的矛盾，为隐私约束下的LLM推荐系统提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local.
