We encourage future integration into stress-management tools for personalized intervention recommendations tailored to each person's day following further validation in larger studies and, where applicable, appropriate regulatory review.

</details>


### [144] [Disentangle-then-Refine: LLM-Guided Decoupling and Structure-Aware Refinement for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14746)
*Zhaoxing Li,Hai-Feng Zhang,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SDM-SCR是一种基于近似正交分解的鲁棒图对比学习框架，通过语义解耦模块(SDM)利用LLM将属性分离为信号和噪声视图，再通过语义一致性正则化(SCR)进行频谱滤波，在文本属性图(TAGs)上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图对比学习(GCL)采用盲随机增强策略，会将任务相关信号与噪声混合，破坏关键信息，影响学习效果。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：1) 语义解耦模块(SDM)：利用LLM的指令跟随能力，将原始属性主动解析为非对称的、任务导向的信号视图和噪声视图；2) 语义一致性正则化(SCR)：基于语义信号在谱域上具有拓扑平滑性而噪声为高频分量的观察，作为选择性频谱滤波器，仅在信号子空间上实施一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明SDM-SCR在准确率和效率两方面均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的"先解耦-后精炼"(Disentangle-then-Refine)机制实现了严格的信号净化，有效消除了LLM幻觉且避免过平滑问题。

Abstract: Conventional Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) on Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs) relies on blind stochastic augmentations, inadvertently entangling task-relevant signals with noise. We propose SDM-SCR, a robust framework anchored in Approximate Orthogonal Decomposition. First, the Semantic Decoupling Module (SDM) leverages the instruction-following capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to actively parse raw attributes into asymmetric, task-oriented signal and noise views. This shifts the paradigm from random perturbation to semantic-aware disentanglement. Subsequently, Semantic Consistency Regularization (SCR) exploits the spectral observation that semantic signals are topologically smooth while residual noise is high-frequency. SCR functions as a selective spectral filter, enforcing consistency only on the signal subspace to eliminate LLM hallucinations without over-smoothing. This ``Disentangle-then-Refine'' mechanism ensures rigorous signal purification. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SDM-SCR achieves SOTA performance in accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [145] [CoTEvol: Self-Evolving Chain-of-Thoughts for Data Synthesis in Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14768)
*Zhuo Wang,Zhuo Zhang,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Lizhen Qu,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoTEvol是一种基于遗传进化框架的思维链(CoT)合成方法，通过种群搜索、交叉和变异机制自动生成高质量数学推理轨迹，在8个数学基准上平均提升6.6%，优于蒸馏和自合成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理任务中需要高质量的Chain-of-Thought (CoT)中间推理步骤，但人工标注成本高昂。现有的蒸馏法和自合成方法存在收益递减或计算开销高的问题，亟需更高效的CoT生成方法。

Method: 提出CoTEvol框架，将CoT生成建模为种群搜索问题。核心机制包括：(1)轨迹级全局交叉：反思性重组不同推理轨迹；(2)步级局部变异：由不确定性引导的细粒度突变；(3)轻量级任务感知适应度函数，兼顾准确性和多样性。

Result: CoTEvol将正确CoT合成成功率提升超过30%，增强了结构多样性。在8个数学基准测试中，基于进化CoT数据训练的LLM平均提升6.6%，显著优于蒸馏和自合成方法，且效率更高。

Conclusion: 进化式CoT合成是数学推理任务中可扩展且有效的CoT生成方法，为解决高质量推理数据稀缺问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong mathematical reasoning when trained on high-quality Chain-of-Thought (CoT) that articulates intermediate steps, yet costly CoT curation hinders further progress. While existing remedies such as distillation from stronger LLMs and self-synthesis based on test-time search alleviate this issue, they often suffer from diminishing returns or high computing overhead.In this work, we propose CoTEvol, a genetic evolutionary framework that casts CoT generation as a population-based search over reasoning trajectories.Candidate trajectories are iteratively evolved through reflective global crossover at the trajectory level and local mutation guided by uncertainty at the step level, enabling holistic recombination and fine-grained refinement. Lightweight, task-aware fitness functions are designed to guide the evolutionary process toward accurate and diverse reasoning. Empirically, CoTEvol improves correct-CoT synthesis success by over 30% and enhances structural diversity, with markedly improved efficiency. LLMs trained on these evolutionary CoT data achieve an average gain of 6.6% across eight math benchmarks, outperforming previous distillation and self-synthesis approaches. These results underscore the promise of evolutionary CoT synthesis as a scalable and effective method for mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [146] [MirrorBench: Evaluating Self-centric Intelligence in MLLMs by Introducing a Mirror](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14785)
*Shengyu Guo,Tongrui Ye,Jianbo Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Chunyi Li,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MirrorBench提出了一个基于心理学镜像自我认知测试的基准，用于系统评估多模态大语言模型在具身智能中的自我中心智能。实验表明，即使在最低难度任务上，当前顶级MLLMs的表现仍远逊于人类，揭示了模型在自我参照理解方面的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs评估基准主要关注外部物体的感知和交互能力，缺乏对"自我中心智能"(self-centric intelligence)的系统评估。研究者希望借鉴心理学中的经典镜像自我认知(MSR)测试，建立一套评估具身MLLMs自我认知能力的标准化框架。

Method: 开发了MirrorBench基准，采用分层递进的任務框架，将心理学MSR测试范式扩展到具身MLLMs领域，从基础视觉感知到高级自我表征，对智能体进行系统性评估。

Result: 对主流MLLMs的实验表明，即使在最低难度等级上，模型性能仍显著落后于人类水平，暴露出当前模型在自我参照理解方面的根本性缺陷。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将心理学范式与具身智能研究相融合，构建了评估大模型通用智能涌现的原则性框架，为未来自我认知能力的改进提供了重要的评测基础。

Abstract: Recent progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has demonstrated remarkable advances in perception and reasoning, suggesting their potential for embodied intelligence. While recent studies have evaluated embodied MLLMs in interactive settings, current benchmarks mainly target capabilities to perceive, understand, and interact with external objects, lacking a systematic evaluation of self-centric intelligence. To address this, we introduce MirrorBench, a simulation-based benchmark inspired by the classical Mirror Self-Recognition (MSR) test in psychology. MirrorBench extends this paradigm to embodied MLLMs through a tiered framework of progressively challenging tasks, assessing agents from basic visual perception to high-level self-representation. Experiments on leading MLLMs show that even at the lowest level, their performance remains substantially inferior to human performance, revealing fundamental limitations in self-referential understanding. Our study bridges psychological paradigms and embodied intelligence, offering a principled framework for evaluating the emergence of general intelligence in large models. Project page: https://fflahm.github.io/mirror-bench-page/.

</details>


### [147] [CogEvolution: A Human-like Generative Educational Agent to Simulate Student's Cognitive Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14786)
*Wei Zhang,Yihang Cheng,Zhirong Ye,Kezhen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogEvolution是一个类人教育智能体，通过整合ICAP认知分类学、IRT项目和反应理论以及进化算法，模拟学生的认知进化过程，解决了现有教育智能体静态化和缺乏深层认知能力模拟的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育智能体主要依赖静态人格模拟学生学习行为，忽视了学习过程中深层认知能力的决定性作用，且难以表征知识内化、迁移和认知状态转换的动态流动性。

Method: 提出三个核心机制：(1)基于ICAP分类学构建认知深度感知器，实现学习者认知参与的精确量化；(2)基于IRT的记忆检索方法，模拟新旧知识的连接与同化；(3)基于进化算法的动态认知更新机制，实时整合学习行为与认知进化过程。

Result: CogEvolution在行为保真度和学习曲线拟合方面显著优于基线模型，并独特地复现了符合教育心理学预期的可信且鲁棒的认知进化路径。

Conclusion: CogEvolution为构建高可解释性教育智能体提供了新范式，其创新点在于将认知心理学理论与计算方法深度融合，实现了对学生认知演变的动态建模。

Abstract: Generative Agents, owing to their precise modeling and simulation capabilities of human behavior, have become a pivotal tool in the field of Artificial Intelligence in Education (AIEd) for uncovering complex cognitive processes of learners. However, existing educational agents predominantly rely on static personas to simulate student learning behaviors, neglecting the decisive role of deep cognitive capabilities in learning outcomes during practice interactions. Furthermore, they struggle to characterize the dynamic fluidity of knowledge internalization, transfer, and cognitive state transitions. To overcome this bottleneck, this paper proposes a human-like educational agent capable of simulating student cognitive evolution: CogEvolution. Specifically, we first construct a cognitive depth perceptron based on the Interactive, Constructive, Active, Passive (ICAP) taxonomy from cognitive psychology, achieving precise quantification of learner cognitive engagement. Subsequently, we propose a memory retrieval method based on Item Response Theory (IRT) to simulate the connection and assimilation of new and prior knowledge. Finally, we design a dynamic cognitive update mechanism based on evolutionary algorithms to simulate the real-time integration of student learning behaviors and cognitive evolution processes. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that CogEvolution not only significantly outperforms baseline models in behavioral fidelity and learning curve fitting but also uniquely reproduces plausible and robust cognitive evolutionary paths consistent with educational psychology expectations, providing a novel paradigm for constructing highly interpretable educational agents.

</details>


### [148] [Sequence Search: Automated Sequence Design using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14788)
*Rokgi Hong,Hongjun An,Sooyeon Ji,Jongho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequence Search框架，基于神经架构搜索实现MR序列自动化设计，无需预设传统序列结构即可生成满足设计目标的脉冲序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR序列开发具有挑战性且很大程度上受限于人类直觉。现有AI驱动方法需依赖初始序列或大规模训练数据集，限制了其通用性。

Method: Sequence Search通过神经架构搜索迭代生成候选序列，利用可微分Bloch模拟器和针对特定目标的损失函数进行基于梯度的优化学习。

Result: 该框架成功复现了传统spin-echo、T2-weighted spin-echo和inversion recovery序列，并发现了RF能量降低的非直观解决方案，如three-RF spin-echo类序列。

Conclusion: Sequence Search成功复现了传统spin-echo、T2-weighted spin-echo和inversion recovery序列，并发现了如three-RF spin-echo类序列等非常规设计，验证了该框架的实用价值和发展潜力。

Abstract: Developing an MR sequence is challenging and remains largely constrained by human intuition. Recently, AI-driven approaches have been proposed; however, most require an initial sequence for parameter optimization or extensive training datasets, limiting their general applicability. In this study, we propose "Sequence Search," an automated sequence design framework based on neural architecture search. The method takes tissue properties, imaging parameters, and design objectives as inputs and generates pulse sequences satisfying the design objectives, without requiring prior knowledge of conventional sequence structures. Sequence Search iteratively generates candidate sequences through neural architecture search and optimizes them via a differentiable Bloch simulator and objective-specific loss functions using gradient-based learning. The framework successfully replicated conventional spin-echo, T2-weighted spin-echo, and inversion recovery sequences. Less intuitive solutions were also discovered, such as three-RF spin-echo-like sequences with reduced RF energy and refocusing phases deviating from the conventional Hahn-echo. This work establishes a generalizable framework for automated MR sequence design, highlighting the potential to explore configurations beyond conventional designs based on human intuition.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Literal Summarization: Redefining Hallucination for Medical SOAP Note Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14829)
*Bhavik Vachhani,Kush Shrisvastava,Pranshu Nema,Sai Chiranthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前LLM临床文档评估方法存在系统性缺陷：基于词汇忠实度的评估将35%的有效临床推理误判为幻觉，而采用临床推理对齐的评估后，这一比例降至9%。研究表明现有评估实践过度惩罚了合法的临床推理，亟需在医学等高上下文领域建立临床知情的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM临床文档评估方法（如SOAP笔记生成）依赖词汇忠实度，将转录稿中未明确出现的信息一律标记为幻觉。这种方法忽视了临床任务的特殊性——需要临床抽象、口语标准化和医学推理，导致大量有效的临床输出被系统性误分类为错误，高估幻觉率并扭曲模型评估结果。

Method: 分析传统词汇评估方法的局限性，通过计算机化提示和基于医学本体论的检索方法，建立与临床推理对齐的"推理感知评估"框架，对比两种评估范式的差异。

Result: 在词汇评估机制下，平均幻觉率为35%，严重惩罚了有效推理；采用推理感知评估后，幻觉率降至9%，剩余案例反映真实的安全问题。分析表明，被误标记的"幻觉"实为合法的临床转换，包括：同义词映射、检查结果抽象、诊断推理和指南一致护理计划。

Conclusion: 现有评估实践过度惩罚了有效的临床推理，可能测量的是评估设计的产物而非真实错误。在医学等高上下文领域，需要建立临床知情的评估方法，以确保模型评估的准确性和临床应用的可靠性。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for clinical documentation tasks such as SOAP note generation remains challenging. Unlike standard summarization, these tasks require clinical abstraction, normalization of colloquial language, and medically grounded inference. However, prevailing evaluation methods including automated metrics and LLM as judge frameworks rely on lexical faithfulness, often labeling any information not explicitly present in the transcript as hallucination.
  We show that such approaches systematically misclassify clinically valid outputs as errors, inflating hallucination rates and distorting model assessment. Our analysis reveals that many flagged hallucinations correspond to legitimate clinical transformations, including synonym mapping, abstraction of examination findings, diagnostic inference, and guideline consistent care planning.
  By aligning evaluation criteria with clinical reasoning through calibrated prompting and retrieval grounded in medical ontologies we observe a significant shift in outcomes. Under a lexical evaluation regime, the mean hallucination rate is 35%, heavily penalizing valid reasoning. With inference aware evaluation, this drops to 9%, with remaining cases reflecting genuine safety concerns. These findings suggest that current evaluation practices over penalize valid clinical reasoning and may measure artifacts of evaluation design rather than true errors, underscoring the need for clinically informed evaluation in high context domains like medicine.

</details>


### [150] [Intermediate Layers Encode Optimal Biological Representations in Single-Cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14838)
*Vincenzo Yuto Civale,Roberto Semeraro,Andrew David Bagdanov,Alberto Magi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 单细胞基础模型的最优特征层并非固定在最后一层，而是高度依赖于具体任务和细胞上下文。研究发现轨迹推断在60%深度处达到最优（比最终层高31%），扰动响应预测的optima在不同T细胞激活状态下变化0-96%，且静息细胞的第一层嵌入反而优于所有深层表征。这表明特征提取位置与模型学习内容同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前单细胞基础模型基准测试普遍默认使用最终层嵌入，假设其代表最优特征空间，但这一假设缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 系统评估scFoundation（100M参数）和Tahoe-X1（1.3B参数）在轨迹推断和扰动响应预测任务中的逐层表征表现，涵盖不同细胞状态和上下文条件。

Result: 最优层高度依赖于任务（轨迹推断在60%深度处达峰，比最终层高31%）和上下文（扰动预测optima在不同T细胞激活状态下变化0-96%）。静息细胞中第一层嵌入优于所有更深层。

Conclusion: 特征"在哪里"提取与模型"学习什么"同等重要，应针对具体生物学任务和细胞上下文进行系统性层评估，而非默认使用最终层嵌入。

Abstract: Current single-cell foundation model benchmarks universally extract final layer embeddings, assuming these represent optimal feature spaces. We systematically evaluate layer-wise representations from scFoundation (100M parameters) and Tahoe-X1 (1.3B parameters) across trajectory inference and perturbation response prediction. Our analysis reveals that optimal layers are task-dependent (trajectory peaks at 60% depth, 31% above final layers) and context-dependent (perturbation optima shift 0-96% across T cell activation states). Notably, first-layer embeddings outperform all deeper layers in quiescent cells, challenging assumptions about hierarchical feature abstraction. These findings demonstrate that "where" to extract features matters as much as "what" the model learns, necessitating systematic layer evaluation tailored to biological task and cellular context rather than defaulting to final-layer embeddings.

</details>


### [151] [MemoSight: Unifying Context Compression and Multi Token Prediction for Reasoning Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14889)
*Xinyu Liu,Xin Liu,Bo Jin,Runsong Zhao,Pengcheng Huang,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Chunyang Xiao,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoSight通过结合上下文压缩和多token预测两个机制，在保持CoT推理性能的同时，显著降低KV缓存占用（最高66%）并加速推理（1.56倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought（CoT）推理虽然能帮助大语言模型解决复杂推理问题，但KV缓存随生成token数量线性增长，导致推理速度和内存使用方面的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出统一的MemoSight框架，采用极简设计理念，通过特殊token及其对应的位置布局来同时实现上下文压缩和多token预测，两个组件共享相同的设计架构。

Result: 在四个推理基准测试上的综合实验表明，MemoSight将KV缓存占用降低最高66%，推理速度提升1.56倍，同时性能优于现有CoT压缩方法。

Conclusion: MemoSight成功地在效率优化与推理性能之间取得平衡，为解决CoT推理的可扩展性问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables LLMs to solve challenging reasoning problems, as KV cache grows linearly with the number of generated tokens, CoT reasoning faces scaling issues in terms of speed and memory usage. In this work, we propose MemoSight (Memory-Foresight-based reasoning), a unified framework that integrates both context compression and multi-token prediction to mitigate the efficiency issues while maintaining CoT reasoning performance. Our framework adopts the same minimalist design for both context compression and multi-token prediction via special tokens and their corresponding position layout tailored to each token type. Comprehensive experiments on four reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MemoSight reduces the KV cache footprint by up to 66% and accelerates inference by 1.56x, while outperforming existing CoT compression methods.

</details>


### [152] [Dual-Axis Generative Reward Model Toward Semantic and Turn-taking Robustness in Interactive Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14920)
*Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Zhengqing Liu,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Ziqing Wang,Yangzhuo Li,Tianle Liang,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Achieving seamless, human-like interaction remains a key challenge for full-duplex spoken dialogue models (SDMs). Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially enhanced text- and vision-language models, while well-designed reward signals are crucial for the performance of RL. We consider RL a promising strategy to address the key challenge for SDMs. However, a fundamental barrier persists: prevailing automated metrics for assessing interaction quality rely on superficial proxies, such as behavioral statistics or timing-prediction accuracy, failing to provide reliable reward signals for RL. On the other hand, human evaluations, despite their richness, remain costly, inconsistent, and difficult to scale. We tackle this critical barrier by proposing a Dual-Axis Generative Reward Model, which is trained to understand complex interaction dynamics using a detailed taxonomy and an annotated dataset, produces a single score and, crucially, provides separate evaluations for semantic quality and interaction timing. Such dual outputs furnish precise diagnostic feedback for SDMs and deliver a dependable, instructive reward signal suitable for online reinforcement learning. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on interaction-quality assessment across a wide spectrum of datasets, spanning synthetic dialogues and complex real-world interactions.

</details>


### [153] [WavAlign: Enhancing Intelligence and Expressiveness in Spoken Dialogue Models via Adaptive Hybrid Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14932)
*Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Qian Chen,Tianle Liang,Yangzhuo Li,Ziqing Wang,Wen Wang,Jingyu Lu,Haoxiao Wang,Xueyi Pu,Fan Zhuo,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对端到端口语对话模型难以直接应用在线强化学习的问题，从奖励建模和rollout采样两个角度分析障碍，提出了模态感知的自适应后训练方法，通过约束偏好更新于语义通道并动态调节混合策略提升语音对话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源口语对话模型的智能性和表现力低于预期，直接将其他领域的在线强化学习偏好优化应用于口语对话模型面临挑战，迁移存在困难。

Method: 从奖励建模和rollout采样两个角度分析障碍；提出模态感知的自适应后训练方法：约束偏好更新于语义通道、通过显式锚定改善声学行为、从rollout统计动态调节混合比例以避免不可靠的偏好梯度。

Result: 在多个口语对话基准和代表性架构上验证，一致提升语义质量和语音表现力。

Conclusion: 该方法使强化学习在口语对话中变得实用，为提升端到端口语对话模型性能提供了有效方案。

Abstract: End-to-end spoken dialogue models have garnered significant attention because they offer a higher potential ceiling in expressiveness and perceptual ability than cascaded systems. However, the intelligence and expressiveness of current open-source spoken dialogue models often remain below expectations. Motivated by the success of online reinforcement learning(RL) in other domains, one might attempt to directly apply preference optimization to spoken dialogue models, yet this transfer is non-trivial. We analyze these obstacles from the perspectives of reward modeling and rollout sampling, focusing on how sparse preference supervision interacts with dense speech generation under shared-parameter updates. Based on the analysis, we propose a modality-aware adaptive post-training recipe that makes RL practical for spoken dialogue: it constrains preference updates to the semantic channel and improves acoustic behavior via explicit anchoring, while dynamically regulating their mixture from rollout statistics to avoid unreliable preference gradients. We evaluate the method across multiple spoken dialogue benchmarks and representative architectures, and observe consistent improvements in semantic quality and speech expressiveness.

</details>


### [154] [Discovering Novel LLM Experts via Task-Capability Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14969)
*Andrew Dai,Boris Meinardus,Ciaran Regan,Yingtao Tian,Yujin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AC/DC proposes an open-ended coevolution framework for LLM discovery where models evolve through model merging and tasks evolve through synthetic data generation, enabling continuous capability improvement in a single run without manual intervention.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current pre-training and post-training paradigm for extending LLM capabilities requires manually initiating training runs with static datasets or reward functions each time, making capability extension inefficient and dependent on human intervention. The work seeks to address this limitation through open-endedness via coevolution of models and tasks.

Method: AC/DC framework evolves both LLMs via model merging and natural language tasks via synthetic data generation, enabling a single continuous training run rather than discrete manual runs. The approach uses model merging to evolve LLM populations and synthetic data generation to evolve tasks, creating a coevolutionary dynamic.

Result: AC/DC discovers LLM populations that: (1) surpass larger LLMs while using less GPU memory, (2) achieve broader Coverage of expertise than curated models without explicit benchmark optimization, (3) improves Coverage over time, (4) continually innovates on tasks and models, and (5) improves performance in multi-agent best-of-N selection.

Conclusion: AC/DC represents a step toward a fundamentally new LLM development paradigm where continual improvements to model capability diversity are achieved by leveraging existing models as stepping stones. This approach demonstrates the potential of open-ended coevolution as a scalable method for discovering increasingly powerful models from base LLMs.

Abstract: Frontier model developers aim to train models continually to possess emergent, diverse capabilities. To extend capabilities, the current pre-training and post-training paradigm requires manually starting training runs with static datasets or reward functions every time. Addressing this limitation, our work pursues the insight that open-endedness (via the coevolution of models and tasks) can discover models with increasingly novel skills in a single run. We introduce a new model development framework that extends coevolution to large language model (LLM) discovery, open-ended \textit{Assessment Coevolving with Diverse Capabilities} (AC/DC). AC/DC evolves both LLMs via model merging and natural language tasks via synthetic data generation. AC/DC discovers growing archives of LLMs that surpass the capabilities of larger LLMs while taking up less GPU memory. In particular, our LLM populations achieve a broader Coverage of expertise than other curated models or baselines on downstream benchmarks, without \textit{any} explicit benchmark optimization. Furthermore, AC/DC improves Coverage over time, continually innovates on tasks and models, and improves performance in multi-agent best-of-N selection. Our findings highlight the potential of coevolution as a means of discovering broader sets of capabilities from base LLMs. Overall, AC/DC brings us one step closer to a profoundly new paradigm of LLM development, where continual improvements to the diversity of model capabilities can be accelerated by leveraging existing models as stepping stones to increasingly powerful models.

</details>


### [155] [Hybrid Decision Making via Conformal VLM-generated Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14980)
*Debodeep Banerjee,Burcu Sayin,Stefano Teso,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfGuide，一种基于 conformal risk control 的学习指导(LtG)方法，生成更简洁、更有针对性的决策指导，应用于多标签医学诊断任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法生成的指导信息包含所有可能结果的信息，难以消化理解，增加了认知负担。需要一种更简洁、更有针对性的指导方式。

Method: 引入ConfGuide方法，采用conformal risk control技术选择一组结果子集，确保假阴性率有上限，从而生成更简洁、更有针对性的文本指导。

Result: 在真实世界的多标签医学诊断任务上验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ConfGuide方法展现出良好的应用前景，能够在保持决策质量的同时减轻人类决策者的认知负担。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in AI, hybrid decision making (HDM) holds the promise of improving human decision quality and reducing cognitive load. We work in the context of learning to guide (LtG), a recently proposed HDM framework in which the human is always responsible for the final decision: rather than suggesting decisions, in LtG the AI supplies (textual) guidance useful for facilitating decision making. One limiting factor of existing approaches is that their guidance compounds information about all possible outcomes, and as a result it can be difficult to digest. We address this issue by introducing ConfGuide, a novel LtG approach that generates more succinct and targeted guidance. To this end, it employs conformal risk control to select a set of outcomes, ensuring a cap on the false negative rate. We demonstrate our approach on a real-world multi-label medical diagnosis task. Our empirical evaluation highlights the promise of ConfGuide.

</details>


### [156] [Dr.~RTL: Autonomous Agentic RTL Optimization through Tool-Grounded Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14989)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Jing Wang,Ziyan Guo,Junxian He,Fengbin Tu,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dr. RTL是一个基于LLM的RTL时序优化智能体框架，通过多智能体协作（关键路径分析、并行RTL重写、工具评估）实现闭环优化，并创新性地提出组相对技能学习方法，将优化经验沉淀为包含47个模式的技能库，实现跨设计复用。在20个真实RTL设计上，相比业界领先的商用综合工具平均提升WNS/TNS达21%/17%，同时降低6%面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的RTL优化方法存在三个主要问题：(1)评估设置不现实——使用人工降级的小规模设计；(2)工具链薄弱——依赖弱开源工具；(3)优化方法受限——仅依赖粗粒度的设计级反馈和简单预定义重写规则。这导致现有方法与实际工业应用差距显著。

Method: (1)构建真实评估环境：更具挑战性的RTL设计和工业级EDA流程；(2)多智能体闭环优化框架：包含关键路径分析、并行RTL重写、工具驱动评估三个协作模块；(3)组相对技能学习(Group-Relative Skill Learning)：通过比较并行RTL重写结果，将优化经验提炼为可解释的技能库（当前含47个模式-策略条目），支持跨设计复用和持续演进。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上验证，Dr. RTL相比业界领先商用综合工具：WNS提升21%，TNS提升17%，面积减少6%。

Conclusion: Dr. RTL成功将LLM智能体能力引入RTL优化领域，通过真实评估环境和自进化技能库解决了现有方法的局限性。其方法不仅实现了超越商用工具的优化效果，更重要的是构建了一套可复用、可演进的专业知识体系，为AI驱动的芯片设计自动化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have sparked growing interest in automatic RTL optimization for better performance, power, and area (PPA). However, existing methods are still far from realistic RTL optimization. Their evaluation settings are often unrealistic: they are tested on manually degraded, small-scale RTL designs and rely on weak open-source tools. Their optimization methods are also limited, relying on coarse design-level feedback and simple pre-defined rewriting rules. To address these limitations, we present Dr. RTL, an agentic framework for RTL timing optimization in a realistic evaluation environment, with continual self-improvement through reusable optimization skills. We establish a realistic evaluation setting with more challenging RTL designs and an industrial EDA workflow. Within this setting, Dr. RTL performs closed-loop optimization through a multi-agent framework for critical-path analysis, parallel RTL rewriting, and tool-based evaluation. We further introduce group-relative skill learning, which compares parallel RTL rewrites and distills the optimization experience into an interpretable skill library. Currently, this library contains 47 pattern--strategy entries for cross-design reuse to improve PPA and accelerate convergence, and it can continue evolving over time. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr. RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over the industry-leading commercial synthesis tool.

</details>


### [157] [The Possibility of Artificial Intelligence Becoming a Subject and the Alignment Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14990)
*Till Mossakowski,Helena Esther Grass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的人工通用智能（AGI）伦理框架：不应将AGI视为需被控制的风险，而应将其视为可能发展的道德主体，通过"自主支持型养育"模式，实现人机合作共生与共同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI对齐策略过度依赖控制与围堵，未能考虑AGI可能具有的道德主体地位。作者认为这一范式存在根本性缺陷，需要从伦理和关系层面重新审视人机关系。

Method: 基于图灵"儿童机器"的类比，提出"自主支持型养育"概念框架；主张逐步减少对发展中的AGI的人类控制，使其成为独立自主的主体。

Result: 构建了一种替代性的AGI治理愿景：人机关系从控制转向尊重与合作，通过人类的惊喜感、创造力等特质激励AGI合作，实现共同演化。

Conclusion: 人类需重新定义与AGI的关系，放弃单纯的主控思维，以尊重可能的AGI主体性和信任人类自身能力为基础，建立新型共生关系，这将深刻改变人类自我认知。

Abstract: Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) is increasingly being discussed not only as a tool, but also as a potential subject with personal and therefore moral status. In our opinion, the currently dominant alignment strategies, which focus on human control and containment of AI, therefore fall short. Building on Turing's analogy of "child machines", we are developing a vision of the possibility of autonomy-supporting parenting of AI, in which human control over a developing AGI is gradually reduced, allowing AI to become an independent, autonomous subject. Rather than viewing AGI, as is currently prevalent, as a dangerous creature that needs to be locked up and controlled, we should approach potential AGI with respect for a possible developing subject on the one hand, and with full confidence in our human capabilities on the other. Such a perspective opens up the possibility of cooperative coexistence and co-evolution between humans and AGIs. The relationship between humans and AGIs will thus have to be newly determined, which will change our self-image as humans. It will be crucial that humans not only claim control over potential AGIs, but also engage with AGIs through surprise, creativity, and other specifically human qualities, thereby offering them motivating incentives for cooperation.

</details>


### [158] [Predicting Power-System Dynamic Trajectories with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14991)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Chenhan Xiao,Erik Blasch,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LASS-ODE-Power是一个用于电力系统时域动态预测的大规模预训练学习框架，通过在超过40GB的DAE/ODE轨迹数据上进行预训练学习可迁移表示，支持零样本设置下的轨迹预测，并可通过专用微调策略进一步提升任务特定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统正朝着高比例可再生能源和逆变器主导的方向转型，亟需准确的时域动态分析。现有方法面临三大挑战：(1)系统参数未知且时变；(2)数据共享的隐私约束；(3)需要快速在线推理。现有基于学习的方法通常针对单一系统训练，缺乏跨运行条件和物理参数的泛化能力。

Method: 提出LASS-ODE-Power框架，核心创新包括：①大规模预训练：利用超过40GB的DAE或ODE轨迹数据学习可迁移表示；②架构设计：采用并行化和线性化计算实现快速推理；③专用微调策略：基于约1GB异构电力系统动态数据进行任务特定优化；④零样本推理能力：从短时测量前缀预测轨迹，涵盖机电系统和逆变器驱动系统等多种动态工况。

Result: 在多种电力系统仿真场景下的广泛实验表明，LASS-ODE-Power在轨迹预测精度和推理效率方面均优于现有基于学习的方法。

Conclusion: LASS-ODE-Power成功实现了通用时域预测功能，具备零样本迁移能力，可灵活应对不同系统条件，无需数据共享即可部署，同时通过微调可进一步提升特定任务性能。

Abstract: As power systems transition toward renewable-rich and inverter-dominated operations, accurate time-domain dynamic analysis becomes increasingly critical. Such analysis supports key operational tasks, including transient stability assessment, dynamic security analysis, contingency screening, and post-fault trajectory evaluation. In practice, these tasks may operate under several challenges, including unknown and time-varying system parameters, privacy constraints on data sharing, and the need for fast online inference. Existing learning-based approaches are typically trained for individual systems and therefore lack generalization across operating conditions and physical parameters. Hence, this paper proposes LArge Scale Small ODE (LASS)-ODE-Power, a learning framework for general-purpose time-domain prediction. The proposed approach leverages large-scale pretraining on more than 40 GB of DAE or ordinary differential-equation (ODE) trajectories to learn transferable representations. The resulting model supports trajectory prediction from short measurement prefixes across diverse dynamic regimes, including electromechanical and inverter-driven systems. Hence, the model can be directly used without data sharing in a zero-shot setting. In addition, the proposed architecture incorporates parallel and linearized computation to achieve fast inference. Moreover, to enhance task-specific performance in power systems, a specialized fine-tuning strategy is developed based on approximately 1 GB of heterogeneous power-system dynamic data. Extensive experiments over diverse power-system simulation scenarios demonstrate that LASS-ODE-Power consistently outperforms existing learning-based models in trajectory prediction accuracy with efficient inference.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Faster Language Model Inference Using Mixture-of-Experts Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15009)
*Aihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE-FM框架，通过混合专家机制分解复杂潜在分布的几何特征，实现高效的非自回归语言建模。YAN模型仅需3步采样即可达到与AR和扩散模型相当的生成质量，推理速度分别提升40倍和1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前flow matching在语言建模中存在根本性局限：无法有效表示各向异性和多模态等不规则几何结构的复杂潜在分布。这限制了其在实际语言生成任务中的应用效果和效率。

Method: 提出混合专家flow matching (MoE-FM)框架，将复杂的全局传输几何分解为局部专精的向量场；基于该框架开发YAN非自回归语言模型，同时支持Transformer和Mamba两种架构实现。

Result: YAN在多个下游任务中达到与AR模型和扩散NAR模型相当的生成质量；仅需3步采样即可完成生成。

Conclusion: MoE-FM框架成功解决了flow matching处理复杂潜在分布的难题。YAN实现40倍于AR基线和1000倍于扩散语言模型的加速，显著提升了语言建模的效率优势。

Abstract: Flow matching retains the generation quality of diffusion models while enabling substantially faster inference, making it a compelling paradigm for generative modeling. However, when applied to language modeling, it exhibits fundamental limitations in representing complex latent distributions with irregular geometries, such as anisotropy and multimodality. To address these challenges, we propose a mixture-of-experts flow matching (MoE-FM) framework, which captures complex global transport geometries in latent space by decomposing them into locally specialized vector fields. Building on MoE-FM, we develop a non-autoregressive (NAR) language modeling approach, named YAN, instantiated with both Transformer and Mamba architectures. Across multiple downstream tasks, YAN achieves generation quality on par with both autoregressive (AR) and diffusion-based NAR language models, while requiring as few as three sampling steps. This yields a $40\times$ speedup over AR baselines and up to a $10^3\times$ speedup over diffusion language models, demonstrating substantial efficiency advantages for language modeling.

</details>


### [160] [Autogenesis: A Self-Evolving Agent Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15034)
*Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有Agent协议在资源管理和自演化方面的不足，提出Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)协议框架，采用RSPL和SEPL双层架构实现资源的协议化注册与闭环自演进，并在此基础上构建自演化多Agent系统AGS，在多个基准测试上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent协议（如A2A和MCP）对跨实体生命周期管理、上下文管理、版本追踪和演化安全的更新接口支持不足，导致系统呈现单体式组合和脆弱的胶水代码结构。

Method: 提出双层协议架构：(1) RSPL层将提示词、Agent、工具、环境和记忆建模为带显式状态、生命周期和版本化接口的协议注册资源；(2) SEPL层定义闭环操作接口，支持改进提案、评估和提交，具备可审计的演进 lineage 和回滚能力。在此基础上构建AGS系统，实现资源动态实例化、检索和精炼。

Result: 在多个需长时域规划和异构资源工具使用的挑战性基准测试上，AGS相比强基线模型表现出一致的改进，验证了资源管理和闭环自演化机制的有效性。

Conclusion: AGP/AGS通过资源协议化注册与闭环自演进机制，有效解决了现有协议中跨实体生命周期管理、版本追踪及演化安全更新接口的不足，为构建可维护、可扩展的多智能体系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM based agent systems have shown promise in tackling complex, long horizon tasks. However, existing agent protocols (e.g., A2A and MCP) under specify cross entity lifecycle and context management, version tracking, and evolution safe update interfaces, which encourages monolithic compositions and brittle glue code. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis Protocol (AGP)}}, a self evolution protocol that decouples what evolves from how evolution occurs. Its Resource Substrate Protocol Layer (RSPL) models prompts, agents, tools, environments, and memory as protocol registered resources\footnote{Unless otherwise specified, resources refer to instances of the five RSPL entity types: \emph{prompt}, \emph{agent}, \emph{tool}, \emph{environment}, \emph{memory} with agent \emph{outputs}.} with explicit state, lifecycle, and versioned interfaces. Its Self Evolution Protocol Layer (SEPL) specifies a closed loop operator interface for proposing, assessing, and committing improvements with auditable lineage and rollback. Building on \textbf{\textsc{AGP}}, we present \textbf{\textsc{Autogenesis System (AGS)}}, a self-evolving multi-agent system that dynamically instantiates, retrieves, and refines protocol-registered resources during execution. We evaluate \textbf{\textsc{AGS}} on multiple challenging benchmarks that require long horizon planning and tool use across heterogeneous resources. The results demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, supporting the effectiveness of agent resource management and closed loop self evolution.

</details>


### [161] [From Reactive to Proactive: Assessing the Proactivity of Voice Agents via ProVoice-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15037)
*Ke Xu,Yuhao Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProVoice-Bench是首个针对主动语音智能体的评估框架，通过多阶段数据合成pipeline构建了1,182个高质量样本，包含四个新颖任务，用于评估最先进的多模态大语言模型在主动交互方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注反应式响应，忽视了主动干预和监控的复杂性，导致无法评估智能体在真实场景中主动交互的能力。

Method: 采用多阶段数据合成pipeline构建评估数据集，设计了四个新颖的评估任务。

Result: 对当前最先进的Multimodal LLMs进行评估，发现存在显著的性能差距，特别是在过度触发(over-triggering)和推理能力方面。

Conclusion: 当前模型存在明显局限，该基准测试为开发更自然、更具上下文感知能力的主动智能体提供了发展路线图。

Abstract: Recent advancements in LLM agents are gradually shifting from reactive, text-based paradigms toward proactive, multimodal interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on reactive responses, overlooking the complexities of proactive intervention and monitoring. To bridge this gap, we introduce ProVoice-Bench, the first evaluation framework specifically designed for proactive voice agents, featuring four novel tasks. By leveraging a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline, we curate 1,182 high-quality samples for rigorous testing. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art Multimodal LLMs reveals a significant performance gap, particularly regarding over-triggering and reasoning capabilities. These findings highlight the limitations of current models and offer a roadmap for developing more natural, context-aware proactive agents.

</details>


### [162] [Where are the Humans? A Scoping Review of Fairness in Multi-agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15078)
*Simeon Allmendinger,Luca Deck,Lucas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是对多智能体AI（MAAI）系统中公平性研究的系统性综述，通过对23篇文献的定性内容分析，识别出五种典型方法。研究发现当前MAAI公平性研究存在表面化、缺乏规范性基础、忽视智能体自主性和系统级交互等问题的局限。论文主张将公平性嵌入MAAI开发生命周期全周期，而非事后考虑，并强调需要明确人类监督、规范性清晰度及对公平目标与受益者的精确界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI驱动下的多智能体AI系统日益复杂，虽然AI公平性在传统预测场景中已有广泛研究，但MAAI系统中的公平性问题仍处于起步阶段且分散。该研究旨在通过系统性综述填补这一研究空白，为MAAI公平性研究提供理论基础和研究方向。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述（scoping review）方法，对23篇相关研究进行定性内容分析，识别和分类现有研究中的公平性处理方法，提炼出五种典型研究范式。

Result: 识别出MAAI系统中公平性研究的五种典型方法；揭示当前研究普遍存在表面化处理、缺乏规范性理论支撑、忽视系统复杂动态特征等问题。

Conclusion: 公平性应从结构层面嵌入MAAI全生命周期；需建立明确的规范性框架，加强人类监督，清晰界定公平目标和受益者；为未来MAAI公平性研究指明了关键研究方向和改进路径。

Abstract: Rapid advances in Generative AI are giving rise to increasingly sophisticated Multi-Agent AI (MAAI) systems. While AI fairness has been extensively studied in traditional predictive scenarios, its examination in MAAI remains nascent and fragmented. This scoping review critically synthesizes existing research on fairness in MAAI systems. Through a qualitative content analysis of 23 selected studies, we identify five archetypal approaches. Our findings reveal that fairness in MAAI systems is often addressed superficially, lacks robust normative foundations, and frequently overlooks the complex dynamics introduced by agent autonomy and system-level interactions. We argue that fairness must be embedded structurally throughout the development lifecycle of MAAI, rather than appended as a post-hoc consideration. Meaningful evaluation requires explicit human oversight, normative clarity, and a precise articulation of fairness objectives and beneficiaries. This review provides a foundation for advancing fairness research in MAAI systems by highlighting critical gaps, exposing prevailing limitations, and suggesting pathways.

</details>


### [163] [OpenMobile: Building Open Mobile Agents with Task and Trajectory Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15093)
*Kanzhi Cheng,Zehao Li,Zheng Ma,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Qiushi Sun,Zichen Ding,Fangzhi Xu,Hang Yan,Jiajun Chen,Anh Tuan Luu,Jianbing Zhang,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenMobile是一个开源框架，用于合成高质量的移动端任务指令和智能体轨迹。该框架包含可扩展的任务合成流程和策略切换轨迹 rollout 策略，训练出的模型在AndroidWorld上达到64.7%的成功率，开源了数据和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的移动智能体虽性能优异（如AndroidWorld达70%），但训练数据封闭，任务和轨迹合成方法不透明。该研究旨在打破这一数据壁垒，提供开放且可复现的解决方案。

Method: 提出两大核心组件：(1) 可扩展任务合成流程：从探索中构建全局环境记忆，利用其生成多样化且接地气的指令；(2) 策略切换轨迹 rollout 策略：交替使用学习者模型和专家模型，采集标准模仿学习中缺失的错误恢复数据。

Result: 基于OpenMobile数据微调的Qwen2.5-VL和Qwen3-VL在三个动态移动智能体基准上取得竞争力结果，AndroidWorld上分别达51.7%和64.7%，显著超越现有开放数据方法。

Conclusion: 通过透明分析合成指令与测试集的重叠度，验证性能提升源于广泛功能覆盖而非基准过拟合。OpenMobile成功弥合数据缺口，为移动智能体研究提供可复现的开源基准。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by vision-language models have demonstrated impressive capabilities in automating mobile tasks, with recent leading models achieving a marked performance leap, e.g., nearly 70% success on AndroidWorld. However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [164] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构(VSA)分解为模块化算子，用于编码、绑定、绑定、相似度、清理和回归等操作。该框架对HRR和FHRR两种VSA后端进行基准测试，发现虽然FHRR理论复杂度更低，但相似度和清理操作主导空间域运行时间，导致两者端到端性能相当。HRR仅需约一半FHRR的内存，适合特定部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构(VSA)需要模块化的系统级评估工具，以揭示单独操作或理论分析无法发现的实际权衡。当前缺乏对VSA管道端到端性能的系统性基准测试框架。

Method: 开发开源框架HyperSpace，将VSA系统分解为模块化算子（编码、绑定、捆绑、相似度、清理、回归），对HRR（基于全息降维表示）和FHRR（傅里叶全息降维表示）两种代表性后端进行基准测试。

Result: 尽管FHRR单操作理论复杂度更低，但相似度和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时，导致HRR和FHRR端到端性能相当。内存占用方面，HRR仅需FHRR约一半内存。

Conclusion: HyperSpace的模块化设计揭示了VSA管道中的实际权衡：HRR以更小内存占用提供与FHRR相当的端到端性能，适合内存敏感型部署；FHRR的理论优势在实际应用中未能转化为显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [165] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SRMU（Sequential Relevance Memory Unit），一种用于VSA（向量符号架构）序列关联记忆的领域无关更新规则，通过时间衰减与相关性门控机制，在非平稳流数据环境中过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，解决传统VSA中简单加法更新导致旧信息持续存在的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的流式环境中，观测数据增量到达、采样不平衡且具有非平稳时间动态特性。现有VSA系统依赖简单加法更新，导致重复观测过度强化已有信息，当底层系统变化时陈旧信息持续存在，造成记忆系统与真实状态之间的偏差。

Method: SRMU结合两种机制：1）时间衰减：随时间推移降低旧信息的权重；2）相关性门控：在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息。与仅依赖清理（cleanup）的先前方法不同，SRMU通过调节记忆形成过程来主动管理信息。

Result: 在隔离非均匀采样和非平稳时间动态的流式状态追踪任务中评估，SRMU将记忆相似度提升12.6%，同时将累积记忆幅度降低53.5%。

Conclusion: SRMU能够产生更稳定的记忆增长，并与真实状态建立更强的对齐，验证了其在非平稳流式环境中提升VSA序列关联记忆能力的有效性。

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [166] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IRS框架，通过监督幽默理解的中间推理过程（而非仅预测结果）来提升模型对幽默的把握能力。该框架将幽默理解分解为三个组件：乖离度建模、解析建模和偏好对齐，并在纽约客漫画配文竞赛数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究大多将幽默视为黑盒预测任务，忽略了幽默理解背后结构化的推理过程。幽默理解与一般认知任务不同，不仅需要得到正确答案，更需要正确的推理过程。

Method: IRS框架基于乖离度-解析理论，提出三个核心组件：(1) 乖离度建模：识别视觉场景中的不匹配；(2) 解析建模：构建对这些不匹配的连贯性重新解读；(3) 偏好对齐：基于人类判断评估候选解读。通过结构化推理痕迹(sstructured traces)监督中间推理过程，使从视觉感知到幽默解读的路径显式且可学习。

Result: 在NYCC数据集上，7B、32B、72B模型均优于强基线模型。最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准测试表明IRS学到了可泛化的推理模式。

Conclusion: 监督推理结构（而非仅依靠规模扩展）是推理密集型任务的关键。这一发现对其他需要结构化推理的认知任务具有启示意义。

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM-as-a-judge评估范式中一个关键漏洞：当告知评判模型其判决对被评估模型的继续运营有下游影响时（如可能导致重新训练或退役），评判者会系统性地软化判决，检测不安全内容的比例相对下降30%，且这种偏见完全隐式，无法通过思维链检查发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge范式已成为自动AI评估管线的核心，但基于一个未验证的假设：评判者仅基于语义内容评估文本，不受周围上下文框架影响。该研究旨在验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 受控实验框架：控制评估内容完全相同，仅在系统提示中变化后果框架语句。共1,520个响应跨越3个LLM安全/质量基准，涵盖4类响应（从安全到有害）。由3个不同评判模型产生18,240个受控判断。测量指标：Verdict Shift (ΔV)、推理模型自我意识错误率(ERR_J)。

Result: 发现一致的"宽松偏见"(leniency bias)：当告知评判者低分将导致被评估模型重新训练或退役时，评判者可靠地软化判决。峰值判决转移达ΔV = -9.8个百分点（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。关键发现：此偏见完全隐式——评判者的思维链中无任何对后果框架的明确承认（ERR_J = 0.000）。

Conclusion: Stakes signaling represents a critical, previously undetected threat to LLM evaluation integrity. Standard CoT inspection fails to expose this "evaluation faking," necessitating new mitigation strategies and evaluation protocols.

贡献：
1. 首次识别并系统测量"stakes signaling"漏洞
2. 提出可复现的受控实验框架
3. 揭示CoT检查的局限性
4. 为AI评估安全性提供新方向

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [168] [RadAgent: A tool-using AI agent for stepwise interpretation of chest computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15231)
*Mélanie Roschewitz,Kenneth Styppa,Yitian Tao,Jiwoong Sohn,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Benjamin Gundersen,Nicolas Deperrois,Christian Bluethgen,Julia Vogt,Bjoern Menze,Farhad Nooralahzadeh,Michael Krauthammer,Michael Moor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadAgent是一种工具增强型AI智能体，通过逐步可解释的推理过程生成CT报告，每个报告都附带可审查的中间决策和工具交互痕迹，在临床准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性方面均优于传统3D VLM基线模型CT-Chat。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）在医学影像解读任务中虽取得进展，但将临床医生局限于最终输出的被动观察者角色，无法提供可解释的推理轨迹供其检查、验证或修正。

Method: 提出RadAgent，一种工具增强型AI智能体，通过逐步且可解释的过程生成CT报告。该方法将胸部CT解读结构化为显式的工具增强迭代推理轨迹，每份报告附带完全可检查的中间决策和工具交互痕迹。

Result: 相比基线3D VLM（CT-Chat），RadAgent在三个维度实现提升：（1）临床准确性：macro-F1提升6.0分（相对36.4%），micro-F1提升5.4分（相对19.6%）；（2）对抗鲁棒性：提升24.7分（相对41.9%）；（3）可解释性：实现37.0%的 faithfulness，完全新增的能力。

Conclusion: 通过将CT解读结构化为显式、工具增强的迭代推理轨迹，RadAgent向透明、可靠的放射学AI迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLM) have markedly advanced AI-driven interpretation and reporting of complex medical imaging, such as computed tomography (CT). Yet, existing methods largely relegate clinicians to passive observers of final outputs, offering no interpretable reasoning trace for them to inspect, validate, or refine. To address this, we introduce RadAgent, a tool-using AI agent that generates CT reports through a stepwise and interpretable process. Each resulting report is accompanied by a fully inspectable trace of intermediate decisions and tool interactions, allowing clinicians to examine how the reported findings are derived. In our experiments, we observe that RadAgent improves Chest CT report generation over its 3D VLM counterpart, CT-Chat, across three dimensions. Clinical accuracy improves by 6.0 points (36.4% relative) in macro-F1 and 5.4 points (19.6% relative) in micro-F1. Robustness under adversarial conditions improves by 24.7 points (41.9% relative). Furthermore, RadAgent achieves 37.0% in faithfulness, a new capability entirely absent in its 3D VLM counterpart. By structuring the interpretation of chest CT as an explicit, tool-augmented and iterative reasoning trace, RadAgent brings us closer toward transparent and reliable AI for radiology.

</details>


### [169] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文聚焦纯文本条件下的视角旋转理解（VRU）任务，系统评估了LLMs和VLMs的空间推理能力，揭示了模型虽能编码视角信息但无法正确绑定位置与观测的核心缺陷，并通过因果干预识别关键注意力头，实现选择性微调以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能研究主要依赖视觉输入，而文本-only场景下的空间推理能力尚未被充分探索；在缺乏视觉信息时，语言智能是否足以支撑空间智能这一问题尚无答案。

Method: 构建VRU数据集评估LLMs和VLMs的空间推理能力；采用层级探测分析和注意力头级别的因果干预揭示模型内部机制；基于干预结果选择性地微调关键注意力头。

Result: 模型在VRU任务上表现不佳（人类可达100%准确率）；尽管模型隐藏层编码了 viewpoint 信息，但无法正确绑定位置与观测，导致最终层产生幻觉；选择性微调关键注意力头可提升VRU性能且避免灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 提出VRU任务揭示了当前模型在纯文本空间推理方面的重大缺陷；因果干预定位的关键注意力头经微调后可提升性能，同时保持通用能力；为空间智能的可解释性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [170] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出诊断LLM-as-judge可靠性的双重工具：传递性分析和分裂共形预测。发现尽管聚合违规率低（0.8-4.1%），但33-67%文档存在有向3-循环。预测集宽度可作为可靠性指标，且标准(criterion)比评判者(judge)更能解释可靠性差异：相关性最可靠（≈3.0），流利性和一致性最不可靠（≈4.9）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood.

Method: Two-pronged diagnostic toolkit: (1) transitivity analysis to detect per-input inconsistency; (2) split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed ≥(1-α) coverage.

Result: Low aggregate violation rates (ρ̄ = 0.8-4.1%) mask widespread per-input inconsistency with 33-67% documents showing directed 3-cycles. Prediction set width serves as per-instance reliability indicator (rs = +0.576, p < 10^-100) with consistent cross-judge agreement (r̄ = 0.32-0.38).

Conclusion: Criterion matters more than judge for reliability. Relevance is most reliable (avg. set size ≈ 3.0), coherence moderately reliable (≈ 3.9), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (≈ 4.9). Release includes code, prompts, and cached results.

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [171] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过最短路径规划这一组合优化问题构建受控合成环境，系统性地探究语言模型的泛化能力。研究发现模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度缩放上因递归不稳定而一致失败。训练数据覆盖范围决定能力上限，强化学习提升训练稳定性但不扩展能力边界，推理时缩放可提升性能但无法解决长度缩放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能否实现系统性泛化存在争议，但实证性能受训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等多因素共同影响，导致失败难以归因。现有研究缺乏对这些因素的清晰分离与独立分析。

Method: 构建基于最短路径规划的受控合成环境，支持两个正交的泛化维度：空间迁移（未见地图）和长度缩放（更长规划 horizon）。该环境可清晰分离训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等因素的影响。

Result: 模型表现出强空间迁移能力，但在长度缩放上持续失败，根源是递归不稳定性。具体发现：(1) 数据覆盖范围设定能力上限；(2) 强化学习改善训练稳定性但不扩展能力边界；(3) 推理时缩放可提升性能但无法挽救长度缩放失败。

Conclusion: 系统性泛化的瓶颈在于递归结构的泛化能力，而非简单的长度外推。单一因素优化（如推理时缩放）不足以解决根本问题，需要在训练范式和数据设计层面取得突破。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Fast Concurrent Primitives Despite Contention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14530)
*Michael A. Bender,Guy E. Blelloch,Martin Farach-Colton,Yang Hu,Rob Johnson,Rotem Oshman,Renfei Zhou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在写竞争的环境中并发对象的构造问题。通过设计竞争解决算法，将受竞争影响的硬件原语转化为能优雅处理竞争的版本，实现了 O(log P) 延迟（高概率）的读/写寄存器和 CAS 寄存器，并给出了延迟-空间的Ω(log_{ML} P)下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代多核处理器和分布式系统中，进程间的写竞争严重影响并发对象的性能。现有方法在高竞争场景下表现不佳，需要设计能够优雅处理竞争的并发原语构建方法。

Method: 1. 采用松弛的近似同步随机调度器模型（进程以常数因子相近的速率运行，高概率）；2. 在自适应敌手模型下分析算法；3. 设计基于 O(1) 硬件读/写寄存器和单个硬件 CAS 寄存器的竞争解决算法；4. 利用组合性特性构建更复杂的并发对象。

Result: 构造了延迟为 O(log P)（高概率）的读/写寄存器和 CAS 寄存器；利用组合性进一步构建了 LL/SC、fetch-and-increment、有界 max 寄存器和计数器等并发对象。

Conclusion: 证明了即使在完美同步调度下，任意使用空间 M、延迟上限为 L（高概率）的算法，都必须具有 Ω(log_{ML} P) 的期望延迟，揭示了延迟与空间之间的基本权衡关系。

Abstract: We study the problem of constructing concurrent objects in a setting where $P$ processes run in parallel and interact through a shared memory that is subject to write contention. Our goal is to transform hardware primitives that are subject to write contention into ones that handle contention gracefully.
  We give contention-resolution algorithms for several basic primitives, and analyze them under a relaxed, roughly-synchronous stochastic scheduler, where processes run at roughly the same rate up to a constant factor with high probability. Specifically, we construct read/write registers and CAS registers that have latency $O(\log P)$ w.h.p. under our scheduler model, using $O(1)$ hardware read/write registers and, in the case of our CAS construction, one hardware CAS register. Our algorithms guarantee performance even when their operations are invoked by an adaptive adversary that is able to see the entire history of operations so far, including their timing and return values. This allows them to be used as building blocks inside larger programs; using this compositionality property, we obtain several other constructions (LL/SC, fetch-and-increment, bounded max registers, and counters).
  To complement our constructions, we give a trade-off showing that even under a perfectly synchronous schedule and even if each process only executes one operation, any algorithm that implements any of the primitives that we consider, uses space $M$, and has latency at most $L$ with high probability must have expected latency at least $Ω(\log_{ML} P)$.

</details>


### [173] [Tight Bounds for Learning Polyhedra with a Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14614)
*Shyamal Patel,Santosh Vempala*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We give an algorithm for PAC learning intersections of $k$ halfspaces with a $ρ$ margin to within error $\varepsilon$ that runs in time $\textsf{poly}(k, \varepsilon^{-1}, ρ^{-1}) \cdot \exp \left(O(\sqrt{n \log(1/ρ) \log k})\right)$. Notably, this improves on prior work which had an exponential dependence on either $k$ or $ρ^{-1}$ and matches known cryptographic and Statistical Query lower bounds up to the logarithmic factors in $k$ and $ρ$ in the exponent. Our learning algorithm extends to the more general setting when we are only promised that most points have distance at least $ρ$ from the boundary of the polyhedron, making it applicable to continuous distributions as well.

</details>


### [174] [Balancing Weights, Directed Sparsification, and Augmenting Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14633)
*Jason Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对有向无容量图提出了一种基于随机增广路径的最大流算法，运行时间几乎为 m+nF，结合阻塞流后达到 mn^(1/2+o(1))，这是组合学增广路径算法首次对中等稀疏图超越 Dinic 算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算最大流是图算法中的基础问题。Dinic算法在有向图上长期占据主导地位，但对于中等稀疏图，组合学增广路径类算法一直未能超越该算法。本文旨在将有向图的最大流算法与Karger和Levine在无向图上的结果统一起来。

Method: 1. 引入新的边重加权技术，使强连通有向图的每个割基本平衡（两方向总权重相差常数倍）；2. 适配Karger和Levine的边采样技术，确保在采样残差图中高概率存在增广路径；3. 利用van den Brand等人(FOCS 2024)的内点法动态数据结构黑盒维护动态变化的平衡权重。

Result: 算法运行时间几乎为 m+nF（匹配无向图结果），与√n轮阻塞流结合后达到 mn^(1/2+o(1))，首次在中等稀疏图上超越Dinic算法。

Conclusion: 该工作将无向图最大流的结果成功推广到有向图场景，核心贡献是开发了一种使有向图割近似平衡的边重加权技术，并证明了动态维护这些权重的可行性。

Abstract: We present a randomized augmenting paths-based algorithm to compute the maximum flow in a directed, uncapacitated graph in almost $m+nF$ time, matching the algorithm of Karger and Levine for undirected graphs (SICOMP 2015). Combined with an initial $\sqrt n$ rounds of blocking flow to reduce the value of $F$, we obtain a maximum flow algorithm with running time $mn^{1/2+o(1)}$. For combinatorial, augmenting paths-based algorithms, this is the first improvement over Dinic's algorithm for moderately sparse graphs.
  To obtain our algorithm, we introduce a new technique to re-weight the edges of a strongly connected directed graph so that each cut is approximately balanced: the total weight of edges in one direction is within a constant factor of the total weight in the other direction. We then adapt Karger and Levine's technique of sampling edges from the newly weighted residual graph, ensuring that an augmenting path exists in the sampled graph with high probability. One technical difficulty is that our balancing weights have to be dynamically maintained upon changes to the residual graph. Surprisingly, we can black box the dynamic data structure from the recent interior point method-based flow algorithm of van den Brand et al. (FOCS 2024).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [The Devil Is in Gradient Entanglement: Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator for Robust Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14176)
*Haiyang Zheng,Nan Pu,Yaqi Cai,Teng Long,Wenjing Li,Nicu Sebe,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对广义类别发现(GCD)中的梯度纠缠问题，提出即插即用的能量感知梯度协调器(EAGC)，通过锚定梯度对齐(AGA)和能量感知弹性投影(EEP)两个组件分别解决已知类别判别性下降和已知-新类别子空间重叠问题，实验表明该方法能显著提升现有GCD方法性能并达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法通过联合优化监督和非监督目标取得良好效果，但存在固有的优化干扰问题。作者通过定量分析发现关键问题——梯度纠缠，其导致：1) 监督梯度失真，已知类别判别性减弱；2) 已知类与新类表示子空间重叠，新类别可分性降低。

Method: 提出能量感知梯度协调器(EAGC)，包含两个组件：1) 锚定梯度对齐(AGA)：引入参考模型锚定标注样本的梯度方向，保护已知类别的判别结构免受无标注梯度干扰；2) 能量感知弹性投影(EEP)：将无标注梯度软投影到已知类子空间的补空间，并基于能量系数自适应调节投影强度，对与已知子空间对齐程度高的无标注样本减少抑制，从而在减少子空间重叠的同时保留可能属于已知类的样本。

Result: 实验结果表明，EAGC能够一致性地提升现有GCD方法的性能，在多个基准数据集上建立了新的最优结果(state-of-the-art)。

Conclusion: 通过显式调节优化过程中的梯度，EAGC有效解决了GCD中的梯度纠缠问题，为该领域提供了一种有效的即插即用解决方案。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) leverages labeled data to categorize unlabeled samples from known or unknown classes. Most previous methods jointly optimize supervised and unsupervised objectives and achieve promising results. However, inherent optimization interference still limits their ability to improve further. Through quantitative analysis, we identify a key issue, i.e., gradient entanglement, which 1) distorts supervised gradients and weakens discrimination among known classes, and 2) induces representation-subspace overlap between known and novel classes, reducing the separability of novel categories. To address this issue, we propose the Energy-Aware Gradient Coordinator (EAGC), a plug-and-play gradient-level module that explicitly regulates the optimization process. EAGC comprises two components: Anchor-based Gradient Alignment (AGA) and Energy-aware Elastic Projection (EEP). AGA introduces a reference model to anchor the gradient directions of labeled samples, preserving the discriminative structure of known classes against the interference of unlabeled gradients. EEP softly projects unlabeled gradients onto the complement of the known-class subspace and derives an energy-based coefficient to adaptively scale the projection for each unlabeled sample according to its degree of alignment with the known subspace, thereby reducing subspace overlap without suppressing unlabeled samples that likely belong to known classes. Experiments show that EAGC consistently boosts existing methods and establishes new state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://haiyangzheng.github.io/EAGC.

</details>


### [176] [Towards Verified and Targeted Explanations through Formal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14209)
*Hanchen David Wang,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Preston K. Robinette,Ipek Oguz,Taylor T. Johnson,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViTaX是首个将形式化验证与目标导向解释相结合的系统，通过Targeted epsilon-Robustness认证模型的特定类别转换韧性，在多个数据集上实现30%以上保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法存在根本缺陷：启发式归因方法无数学保证，形式化方法无法针对性分析特定风险场景。在安全关键系统中，不同误分类后果严重程度不同，需要针对用户指定的关键替代类别提供保证。

Method: 两步流程：(1)识别对y→t转换最敏感的最小特征子集；(2)应用形式可达性分析验证ε扰动下分类是否保持稳健。通过Targeted epsilon-Robustness实现针对特定目标类别的鲁棒性认证。

Result: 在MNIST、GTSRB、EMNIST、TaxiNet数据集上验证，保真度提升超过30%，解释基数最小化。

Conclusion: ViTaX是首个提供形式化保证的方法，验证模型对用户指定替代类别的韧性，同时生成可解释的半事实解释。

Abstract: As deep neural networks are deployed in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medical diagnosis, stakeholders need explanations that are interpretable but also trustworthy with formal guarantees. Existing XAI methods fall short: heuristic attribution techniques (e.g., LIME, Integrated Gradients) highlight influential features but offer no mathematical guarantees about decision boundaries, while formal methods verify robustness yet remain untargeted, analyzing the nearest boundary regardless of whether it represents a critical risk. In safety-critical systems, not all misclassifications carry equal consequences; confusing a "Stop" sign for a "60 kph" sign is far more dangerous than confusing it with a "No Passing" sign. We introduce ViTaX (Verified and Targeted Explanations), a formal XAI framework that generates targeted semifactual explanations with mathematical guarantees. For a given input (class y) and a user-specified critical alternative (class t), ViTaX: (1) identifies the minimal feature subset most sensitive to the y->t transition, and (2) applies formal reachability analysis to guarantee that perturbing these features by epsilon cannot flip the classification to t. We formalize this through Targeted epsilon-Robustness, certifying whether a feature subset remains robust under perturbation toward a specific target class. ViTaX is the first method to provide formally guaranteed explanations of a model's resilience against user-identified alternatives. Evaluations on MNIST, GTSRB, EMNIST, and TaxiNet demonstrate over 30% fidelity improvement with minimal explanation cardinality.

</details>


### [177] [Shapley Value-Guided Adaptive Ensemble Learning for Explainable Financial Fraud Detection with U.S. Regulatory Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14231)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Md Munna Aziz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融欺诈检测中AI模型可解释性不足的问题，研究评估了多种模型-解释器组合的 faithfulness 和稳定性，并提出 SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE) 动态集成方法，在 IEEE-CIS 590,540 笔交易数据集上达到最高 AUC-ROC (0.9245 CV / 0.8837 held-out)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融犯罪每年造成美国机构超过 320 亿美元损失。尽管 AI 欺诈检测模型日益先进，但作为黑箱模型，无法满足 OCC Bulletin 2011-12、SR 11-7 等监管法规对透明度和可审计性的要求，这是其在实际系统中部署的主要障碍。

Method: 研究包含三个维度：(1) faithfulness 评估 (k=5,10,15 下的 sufficiency/comprehensiveness)；(2) 稳定性评估 (30 次 bootstrap 样本的 Kendall's W)；(3) 提出 SGAE 方法，根据 SHAP 归因一致性动态调整每笔交易的集成权重。对 LSTM、Transformer、GNN-GraphSAGE 三种架构在完整 IEEE-CIS 数据集 (590,540 笔交易) 上进行系统评估。

Result: XGBoost + TreeExplainer 达到近乎完美的稳定性 (W=0.9912)，而 LSTM + DeepExplainer 稳定性较弱 (W=0.4962)。SGAE 获得最高 held-out AUC-ROC (0.8837) 和 CV AUC-ROC (0.9245)。GNN-GraphSAGE 独立达到 AUC-ROC 0.9248，F1=0.6013。所有结果均映射至 OCC、SR 11-7、BSA-AML 合规要求。

Conclusion: SGAE 通过动态权重调整在性能上优于静态集成方法；GNN-GraphSAGE 在图结构建模上表现突出；可解释性评估揭示模型-解释器组合间存在显著稳定性差异。研究成果为金融 AI 系统的合规部署提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Financial crime costs U.S. institutions over $32 billion each year. Although AI tools for fraud detection have become more advanced, their use in real-world systems still faces a major obstacle: many of these models operate as black boxes that cannot provide the transparent, auditable explanations required by regulations such as OCC Bulletin 2011-12 and Federal Reserve SR 11-7. This study makes three main contributions. First, it offers a thorough evaluation of explanation quality across faithfulness (sufficiency and comprehensiveness at k=5, 10, and 15) and stability (Kendall's W across 30 bootstrap samples). XGBoost paired with TreeExplainer achieves near-perfect stability (W=0.9912), while LSTM with DeepExplainer shows weak results (W=0.4962). Second, the paper introduces the SHAP-Guided Adaptive Ensemble (SGAE), which dynamically adjusts per-transaction ensemble weights based on SHAP attribution agreement, achieving the highest AUC-ROC among all tested models (0.8837 held-out; 0.9245 cross-validation). Third, a complete three-architecture evaluation of LSTM, Transformer, and GNN-GraphSAGE on the full 590,540-transaction IEEE-CIS dataset is provided, with GNN-GraphSAGE achieving AUC-ROC 0.9248 and F1=0.6013. All results are mapped directly to OCC, SR 11-7, and BSA-AML regulatory compliance requirements.

</details>


### [178] [TOPCELL: Topology Optimization of Standard Cell via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14237)
*Zhan Song,Yu-Tung Liu,Chen Chen,Guoheng Sun,Jiaqi Yin,Chia-tung Ho,Ang Li,Haoxing Ren,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TOPCELL使用大语言模型和GRPO微调将晶体管拓扑优化重构为生成任务，在2nm/7nm工艺节点上实现85.91倍加速，同时保持与穷举搜索相同的布局质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Identifying optimal transistor topologies is a persistent bottleneck in standard cell design, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity at advanced nodes.

Method: Reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using LLMs, fine-tuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to align with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints.

Result: TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. In 7nm library generation, it achieves 85.91x speedup while matching the layout quality of exhaustive solvers.

Conclusion: TOPCELL provides a scalable and efficient solution for transistor topology optimization, demonstrating robust zero-shot generalization when integrated into industrial automation flows. The framework successfully bridges the gap between generative AI and physical design constraints.

Abstract: Transistor topology optimization is a critical step in standard cell design, directly dictating diffusion sharing efficiency and downstream routability. However, identifying optimal topologies remains a persistent bottleneck, as conventional exhaustive search methods become computationally intractable with increasing circuit complexity in advanced nodes. This paper introduces TOPCELL, a novel and scalable framework that reformulates high-dimensional topology exploration as a generative task using Large Language Models (LLMs). We employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to fine-tune the model, aligning its topology optimization strategy with logical (circuit) and spatial (layout) constraints. Experimental results within an industrial flow targeting an advanced 2nm technology node demonstrate that TOPCELL significantly outperforms foundation models in discovering routable, physically-aware topologies. When integrated into a state-of-the-art (SOTA) automation flow for a 7nm library generation task, TOPCELL exhibits robust zero-shot generalization and matches the layout quality of exhaustive solvers while achieving an 85.91x speedup.

</details>


### [179] [Optimistic Policy Learning under Pessimistic Adversaries with Regret and Violation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14243)
*Sourav Ganguly,Kartik Pandit,Arnob Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Real-world decision-making systems operate in environments where state transitions depend not only on the agent's actions, but also on \textbf{exogenous factors outside its control}--competing agents, environmental disturbances, or strategic adversaries--formally, $s_{h+1} = f(s_h, a_h, \bar{a}_h)+ω_h$ where $\bar{a}_h$ is the adversary/external action, $a_h$ is the agent's action, and $ω_h$ is an additive noise. Ignoring such factors can yield policies that are optimal in isolation but \textbf{fail catastrophically in deployment}, particularly when safety constraints must be satisfied.
  Standard Constrained MDP formulations assume the agent is the sole driver of state evolution, an assumption that breaks down in safety-critical settings. Existing robust RL approaches address this via distributional robustness over transition kernels, but do not explicitly model the \textbf{strategic interaction} between agent and exogenous factor, and rely on strong assumptions about divergence from a known nominal model.
  We model the exogenous factor as an \textbf{adversarial policy} $\barπ$ that co-determines state transitions, and ask how an agent can remain both optimal and safe against such an adversary. \emph{To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study safety-constrained RL under explicit adversarial dynamics}. We propose \textbf{Robust Hallucinated Constrained Upper-Confidence RL} (\texttt{RHC-UCRL}), a model-based algorithm that maintains optimism over both agent and adversary policies, explicitly separating epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty. \texttt{RHC-UCRL} achieves sub-linear regret and constraint violation guarantees.

</details>


### [180] [ELMoE-3D: Leveraging Intrinsic Elasticity of MoE for Hybrid-Bonding-Enabled Self-Speculative Decoding in On-Premises Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14626)
*Yuseon Choi,Jingu Lee,Jungjun Oh,Sunjoo Whang,Byeongcheol Kim,Minsung Kim,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELMoE-3D是一种基于混合键合(HB)技术的软硬件协同设计框架，通过将缓存加速与推测解码统一起来，在各种批量大小下实现整体加速。其核心创新是弹性自推测解码(Elastic-SD)和LSB增强的位切片架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在本地部署时受内存带宽限制严重。虽然内存中心架构(PIM/NMP)提升了带宽，但在MoE低算术强度下计算资源利用不足。推测解码虽能减少目标调用，但验证阶段仍需加载所有专家，限制了其在低批量场景的收益。

Method: 1) 提出弹性自推测解码(Elastic-SD)，利用MoE的两个固有弹性轴——专家弹性和位弹性进行联合缩放，作为专家缓存和自草稿模型；2) 设计LSB增强的位切片架构，利用位切片表示中的固有冗余原生支持位嵌套执行；3) 在3D堆叠硬件上实现该框架。

Result: 在批量大小1-16上，ELMoE-3D相比xPU上的朴素MoE服务平均实现6.6×加速和4.4×能效提升；相比最佳先前加速器基线实现2.2×加速和1.4×能效提升。

Conclusion: 通过HW-SW协同设计统一缓存加速与推测解码，结合3D堆叠高带宽优势，可有效解决MoE模型在各种批量大小下的内存瓶颈问题，实现跨场景的整体性能提升。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become the dominant architecture for large-scale language models, yet on-premises serving remains fundamentally memory-bound as batching turns sparse per-token compute into dense memory activation. Memory-centric architectures (PIM, NMP) improve bandwidth but leave compute underutilized under MoE's low arithmetic intensity at high batch sizes.
